These results establish OFP as a practical, scalable solution for highly accurate and low-latency robot control.

</details>


### [60] [Robots that redesign themselves through kinematic self-destruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12505)
*Chen Yu,Sam Kriegman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人通过自主的"运动学自毁"机制，在生命周期中主动参与自身设计，从随机组装的躯体开始，识别并移除阻碍运动的冗余连接，从而优化自身形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人都是预先设计好的，缺乏在部署后自我优化的能力。本研究旨在探索机器人能否在生命周期中主动参与自身设计，通过动态调整形态来适应环境并提升性能。

Method: 使用单一自回归序列模型作为通用控制器，在仿真中学习何时以及如何通过"运动学自毁"简化机器人躯体：识别阻碍运动的冗余连接，然后通过剧烈摆动使其在关节处断裂脱落。优化后的策略能够迁移到现实世界并泛化到未见过的运动学结构。

Result: 自主设计的机器人产生的向前运动比随机移除连接或不能移除任何连接的等效策略更有效。该策略成功迁移到现实世界，并能泛化到未见过的运动学结构。

Conclusion: 自主设计机器人可能在某些情况下比预先设计机器人更成功，运动学自毁虽然具有还原性和不可逆性，但可能为广泛机器人提供通用的自适应策略。

Abstract: Every robot built to date was predesigned by an external process, prior to deployment. Here we show a robot that actively participates in its own design during its lifetime. Starting from a randomly assembled body, and using only proprioceptive feedback, the robot dynamically ``sculpts'' itself into a new design through kinematic self-destruction: identifying redundant links within its body that inhibit its locomotion, and then thrashing those links against the surface until they break at the joint and fall off the body. It does so using a single autoregressive sequence model, a universal controller that learns in simulation when and how to simplify a robot's body through self-destruction and then adaptively controls the reduced morphology. The optimized policy successfully transfers to reality and generalizes to previously unseen kinematic trees, generating forward locomotion that is more effective than otherwise equivalent policies that randomly remove links or cannot remove any. This suggests that self-designing robots may be more successful than predesigned robots in some cases, and that kinematic self-destruction, though reductive and irreversible, could provide a general adaptive strategy for a wide range of robots.

</details>


### [61] [Red-Teaming Vision-Language-Action Models via Quality Diversity Prompt Generation for Robust Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12510)
*Siddharth Srikanth,Freddie Liang,Sophie Hsu,Varun Bhatt,Shihan Zhao,Henry Chen,Bryon Tjanaka,Minjune Hwang,Akanksha Saran,Daniel Seita,Aaquib Tabrez,Stefanos Nikolaidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Q-DIG使用质量多样性(QD)技术和视觉语言模型(VLM)生成多样化的对抗性指令，以暴露VLA模型的脆弱性并提高其鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型对语言指令的措辞高度敏感，难以预测其失败情况，需要提高对不同措辞的鲁棒性

Method: Q-DIG整合质量多样性(QD)技术和视觉语言模型(VLM)，可扩展地识别多样化的自然语言任务描述，这些描述能引发失败但仍保持任务相关性

Result: 在多个仿真基准测试中，Q-DIG相比基线方法发现更多样化和有意义的失败模式；微调VLA能提高任务成功率；用户研究显示Q-DIG生成的提示更自然、更人性化；真实世界评估结果与仿真一致

Conclusion: Q-DIG是识别VLA机器人脆弱性和提高其鲁棒性的有前景方法，通过生成多样化对抗指令进行红队测试

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significant potential to enable general-purpose robotic systems for a range of vision-language tasks. However, the performance of VLA-based robots is highly sensitive to the precise wording of language instructions, and it remains difficult to predict when such robots will fail. To improve the robustness of VLAs to different wordings, we present Q-DIG (Quality Diversity for Diverse Instruction Generation), which performs red-teaming by scalably identifying diverse natural language task descriptions that induce failures while remaining task-relevant. Q-DIG integrates Quality Diversity (QD) techniques with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate a broad spectrum of adversarial instructions that expose meaningful vulnerabilities in VLA behavior. Our results across multiple simulation benchmarks show that Q-DIG finds more diverse and meaningful failure modes compared to baseline methods, and that fine-tuning VLAs on the generated instructions improves task success rates. Furthermore, results from a user study highlight that Q-DIG generates prompts judged to be more natural and human-like than those from baselines. Finally, real-world evaluations of Q-DIG prompts show results consistent with simulation, and fine-tuning VLAs on the generated prompts further success rates on unseen instructions. Together, these findings suggest that Q-DIG is a promising approach for identifying vulnerabilities and improving the robustness of VLA-based robots. Our anonymous project website is at qdigvla.github.io.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond Dense Futures: World Models as Structured Planners for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12553)
*Minghao Jin,Mozheng Liao,Mingfei Han,Zhihui Li,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StructVLA：一种基于结构化视觉前瞻的VLA架构，通过预测稀疏的物理意义结构化帧而非密集视觉预测，解决长时程规划漂移问题，提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的VLA架构存在两个主要问题：1）密集未来预测引入视觉冗余和误差累积，导致长时程规划漂移；2）稀疏方法通常使用高层语义子任务或隐式潜在状态，缺乏显式运动学基础，导致规划与低层执行对齐不足。

Method: 提出StructVLA，将生成式世界模型重构为显式结构化规划器。方法包括：1）预测稀疏的物理意义结构化帧，基于内在运动学线索（如夹爪状态转换和运动学转折点）；2）采用两阶段训练范式：首先训练世界模型预测结构化帧，然后优化将结构化前瞻映射到低层动作；3）使用统一的离散令牌词汇表。

Result: 在SimperEnv-WidowX上达到75.0%的平均成功率，在LIBERO上达到94.8%的平均成功率。真实世界部署进一步展示了在基础抓放和复杂长时程任务上的可靠任务完成和鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: StructVLA通过结构化视觉前瞻方法有效解决了视觉冗余、误差累积和规划-执行对齐问题，为机器人操作提供了清晰的物理指导，桥接了视觉规划与运动控制。

Abstract: Recent world-model-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures have improved robotic manipulation through predictive visual foresight. However, dense future prediction introduces visual redundancy and accumulates errors, causing long-horizon plan drift. Meanwhile, recent sparse methods typically represent visual foresight using high-level semantic subtasks or implicit latent states. These representations often lack explicit kinematic grounding, weakening the alignment between planning and low-level execution. To address this, we propose StructVLA, which reformulates a generative world model into an explicit structured planner for reliable control. Instead of dense rollouts or semantic goals, StructVLA predicts sparse, physically meaningful structured frames. Derived from intrinsic kinematic cues (e.g., gripper transitions and kinematic turning points), these frames capture spatiotemporal milestones closely aligned with task progress. We implement this approach through a two-stage training paradigm with a unified discrete token vocabulary: the world model is first trained to predict structured frames and subsequently optimized to map the structured foresight into low-level actions. This approach provides clear physical guidance and bridges visual planning and motion control. In our experiments, StructVLA achieves strong average success rates of 75.0% on SimplerEnv-WidowX and 94.8% on LIBERO. Real-world deployments further demonstrate reliable task completion and robust generalization across both basic pick-and-place and complex long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [63] [From Woofs to Words: Towards Intelligent Robotic Guide Dogs with Verbal Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12574)
*Yohei Hayamizu,David DeFazio,Hrudayangam Mehta,Zainab Altaweel,Jacqueline Choe,Chao Lin,Jake Juettner,Furui Xiao,Jeremy Blackburn,Shiqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发用于导盲机器犬的新型对话系统，利用LLM将导航计划和场景信息语言化，实现人机协作决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为导盲机器犬增加语言能力面临独特挑战：需要在动态变化的环境中实现语言接地，并提高人类使用者的空间意识。现有对话系统无法直接应用于移动机器人导盲场景。

Method: 开发基于LLM的新型对话系统，能够将导航计划和场景信息转化为语言描述。通过语言化策略实现环境信息的有效传达，支持人机协作决策。

Result: 通过人类研究评估不同语言化策略的效果，并通过仿真研究评估导航任务的效率和准确性。验证了系统在协作决策和导航性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 为导盲机器犬设计的语言化对话系统能够有效应对动态环境中的语言接地挑战，提高人机协作的空间意识和导航决策质量，为辅助机器人领域提供了新的交互范式。

Abstract: Assistive robotics is an important subarea of robotics that focuses on the well-being of people with disabilities. A robotic guide dog is an assistive quadruped robot that helps visually impaired people in obstacle avoidance and navigation. Enabling language capabilities for robotic guide dogs goes beyond naively adding an existing dialog system onto a mobile robot. The novel challenges include grounding language in the dynamically changing environment and improving spatial awareness for the human handler. To address those challenges, we develop a novel dialog system for robotic guide dogs that uses LLMs to verbalize both navigational plans and scenes. The goal is to enable verbal communication for collaborative decision-making within the handler-robot team. In experiments, we conducted a human study to evaluate different verbalization strategies and a simulation study to assess the efficiency and accuracy in navigation tasks.

</details>


### [64] [Skill-informed Data-driven Haptic Nudges for High-dimensional Human Motor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12583)
*Ankur Kamboj,Rajiv Ranganathan,Xiaobo Tan,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于数据的技能感知框架，通过POMDP优化高维运动学习任务中的触觉反馈设计，加速学习过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维新颖运动学习任务中，如何设计最优的触觉反馈来加速学习过程是一个挑战。传统启发式方法缺乏理论基础，无法适应个体差异和技能演化过程。

Method: 1. 使用输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型(IOHMM)建模人类运动学习的随机动态，解耦潜在技能演化与可观测运动学输出；2. 将触觉反馈设计问题形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)；3. 推导最小化长期性能成本的最优推动策略，引导学习者走向技能空间的稳健区域。

Result: 通过30人参与的手部外骨骼任务实验验证：1. POMDP策略组相比启发式反馈组和无反馈组，任务表现显著加速；2. 协同分析显示POMDP组更快发现高效的低维运动表征。

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动技能感知框架能够有效设计最优触觉反馈，加速高维运动学习任务中的技能获取过程，促进高效运动表征的形成。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a data-driven skill-informed framework to design optimal haptic nudge feedback for high-dimensional novel motor learning tasks. We first model the stochastic dynamics of human motor learning using an Input-Output Hidden Markov Model (IOHMM), which explicitly decouples latent skill evolution from observable kinematic emissions. Leveraging this predictive model, we formulate the haptic nudge feedback design problem as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). This allows us to derive an optimal nudging policy that minimizes long-term performance cost, implicitly guiding the learner toward robust regions of the skill space. We validated our approach through a human-subject study ($N=30$) using a high-dimensional hand-exoskeleton task. Results demonstrate that participants trained with the POMDP-derived policy exhibited significantly accelerated task performance compared to groups receiving heuristic-based feedback or no feedback. Furthermore, synergy analysis revealed that the POMDP group discovered efficient low-dimensional motor representations more rapidly.

</details>


### [65] [CarPLAN: Context-Adaptive and Robust Planning with Dynamic Scene Awareness for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12607)
*Junyong Yun,Jungho Kim,ByungHyun Lee,Dongyoung Lee,Sehwan Choi,Seunghyeop Nam,Kichun Jo,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CarPLAN：一种基于模仿学习的运动规划框架，通过位移感知预测编码和上下文自适应多专家解码器增强驾驶上下文理解，在nuPlan和Waymax基准测试中实现最先进性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模仿学习的自动驾驶运动规划需要捕捉真实世界驾驶数据中的复杂上下文，并实现上下文自适应决策，而不仅仅是模仿专家轨迹。现有方法在理解驾驶上下文和适应多样化交通场景方面存在不足。

Method: 提出CarPLAN框架：1) 位移感知预测编码(DPE)：通过预测自动驾驶车辆与周围场景元素之间的未来位移向量，增强模型的空间感知能力；2) 上下文自适应多专家解码器(CMD)：基于混合专家框架，根据场景结构动态选择最合适的专家解码器，实现自适应规划。

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，CarPLAN在所有闭环仿真指标上实现了最先进的性能，特别是在Test14-Hard等挑战性场景中表现出鲁棒性。在Waymax基准测试上的额外实验进一步证明了其在不同基准设置下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CarPLAN通过增强驾驶上下文理解和实现自适应规划，显著提升了基于模仿学习的运动规划性能，为复杂动态环境中的自动驾驶提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) is widely used for motion planning in autonomous driving due to its data efficiency and access to real-world driving data. For safe and robust real-world driving, IL-based planning requires capturing the complex driving contexts inherent in real-world data and enabling context-adaptive decision-making, rather than relying solely on expert trajectory imitation. In this paper, we propose CarPLAN, a novel IL-based motion planning framework that explicitly enhances driving context understanding and enables adaptive planning across diverse traffic scenarios. Our contributions are twofold: We introduce Displacement-Aware Predictive Encoding (DPE) to improve the model's spatial awareness by predicting future displacement vectors between the Autonomous Vehicle (AV) and surrounding scene elements. This allows the planner to account for relational spacing when generating trajectories. In addition to the standard imitation loss, we incorporate an augmented loss term that captures displacement prediction errors, ensuring planning decisions consider relative distances from other agents. To improve the model's ability to handle diverse driving contexts, we propose Context-Adaptive Multi-Expert Decoder (CMD), which leverages the Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework. CMD dynamically selects the most suitable expert decoders based on scene structure at each Transformer layer, enabling adaptive and context-aware planning in dynamic environments. We evaluate CarPLAN on the nuPlan benchmark and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all closed-loop simulation metrics. In particular, CarPLAN exhibits robust performance on challenging scenarios such as Test14-Hard, validating its effectiveness in complex driving conditions. Additional experiments on the Waymax benchmark further demonstrate its generalization capability across different benchmark settings.

</details>


### [66] [Autonomous Integration and Improvement of Robotic Assembly using Skill Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12649)
*Peiqi Yu,Philip Huang,Chaitanya Chawla,Guanya Shi,Jiaoyang Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于技能图（Skill Graph）的机器人装配系统自主集成与持续改进框架，将机器人能力组织为基于动词的技能，统一系统配置、执行、评估和学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人装配系统需要大量人工工程工作来集成新任务、适应新环境和随时间改进性能，缺乏自适应和可重用的系统框架。

Method: 使用技能图表示法，将机器人能力组织为基于动词的技能，明确连接语义描述（动词和名词）与可执行策略、前置条件、后置条件和评估器，支持语义级规划和执行落地。

Result: 技能图框架支持快速系统集成、系统化数据收集和闭环性能改进，实现了系统配置、执行、评估和学习在单一表示中的统一。

Conclusion: 技能图为自适应和可重用的机器人装配系统提供了可扩展的途径，代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Robotic assembly systems traditionally require substantial manual engineering effort to integrate new tasks, adapt to new environments, and improve performance over time. This paper presents a framework for autonomous integration and continuous improvement of robotic assembly systems based on Skill Graph representations. A Skill Graph organizes robot capabilities as verb-based skills, explicitly linking semantic descriptions (verbs and nouns) with executable policies, pre-conditions, post-conditions, and evaluators. We show how Skill Graphs enable rapid system integration by supporting semantic-level planning over skills, while simultaneously grounding execution through well-defined interfaces to robot controllers and perception modules. After initial deployment, the same Skill Graph structure supports systematic data collection and closed-loop performance improvement, enabling iterative refinement of skills and their composition. We demonstrate how this approach unifies system configuration, execution, evaluation, and learning within a single representation, providing a scalable pathway toward adaptive and reusable robotic assembly systems. The code is at https://github.com/intelligent-control-lab/AIDF.

</details>


### [67] [TacVLA: Contact-Aware Tactile Fusion for Robust Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12665)
*Kaidi Zhang,Heng Zhang,Zhengtong Xu,Zhiyuan Zhang,Md Rakibul Islam Prince,Xiang Li,Xiaojing Han,Yuhao Zhou,Arash Ajoudani,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacVLA：通过引入触觉模态和接触感知门控机制增强VLA模型在精细操作任务中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在视觉遮挡、精细操作和物理接触任务中表现不佳，需要引入触觉模态来增强模型在接触丰富交互中的能力

Method: 提出TacVLA模型，通过接触感知门控机制选择性激活触觉token，实现自适应多模态融合，避免无关触觉干扰；将融合的视觉、语言和触觉token在Transformer架构中联合处理

Result: 在约束锁定拆卸、箱内拾取和鲁棒性评估中优于基线模型：拆卸任务平均提升20%成功率，箱内拾取提升60%，视觉遮挡场景提升2.1倍

Conclusion: TacVLA通过有效整合触觉模态显著增强了VLA模型在精细操作任务中的性能，特别是在视觉遮挡和接触丰富场景下表现优异

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated significant advantages in robotic manipulation. However, their reliance on vision and language often leads to suboptimal performance in tasks involving visual occlusion, fine-grained manipulation, and physical contact. To address these challenges, we propose TacVLA, a fine-tuned VLA model by incorporating tactile modalities into the transformer-based policy to enhance fine-grained manipulation capabilities. Specifically, we introduce a contact-aware gating mechanism that selectively activates tactile tokens only when contact is detected, enabling adaptive multimodal fusion while avoiding irrelevant tactile interference. The fused visual, language, and tactile tokens are jointly processed within the transformer architecture to strengthen cross-modal grounding during contact-rich interaction. Extensive experiments on constraint-locked disassembly, in-box picking and robustness evaluations demonstrate that our model outperforms baselines, improving the performance by averaging 20% success rate in disassembly, 60% in in-box picking and 2.1x improvement in scenarios with visual occlusion. Videos are available at https://sites.google.com/view/tacvla and code will be released.

</details>


### [68] [Learning Athletic Humanoid Tennis Skills from Imperfect Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12686)
*Zhikai Zhang,Haofei Lu,Yunrui Lian,Ziqing Chen,Yun Liu,Chenghuai Lin,Han Xue,Zicheng Zeng,Zekun Qi,Shaolin Zheng,Qing Luan,Jingbo Wang,Junliang Xing,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LATENT系统从非完美的人类网球动作数据中学习类人机器人的网球技能，实现了稳定击打来球并回击到目标位置，成功部署到Unitree G1机器人上并与人类进行多拍对抗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动员在网球中展现出高度动态和多样化的技能，但将这些行为复现到类人机器人上很困难，主要因为缺乏完美的类人动作数据或网球场景中的人类运动学数据作为参考。

Method: 提出LATENT系统，从非完美的人类运动数据中学习。这些数据只包含捕捉网球基本技能的运动片段，而非完整精确的网球比赛动作序列。通过校正和组合这些数据，学习类人机器人策略，使其能在各种条件下稳定击打来球并回击到目标位置，同时保持自然的运动风格。还提出了一系列鲁棒的仿真到现实迁移设计。

Result: 该方法在真实世界中取得了令人惊讶的结果，能够稳定地与人类玩家进行多拍对抗。成功将策略部署到Unitree G1类人机器人上。

Conclusion: LATENT系统证明了从非完美的人类运动数据中学习类人机器人网球技能的可行性，通过利用网球场景中人类基本技能的先验知识，经过校正和组合，实现了鲁棒的仿真到现实迁移和稳定的多拍对抗能力。

Abstract: Human athletes demonstrate versatile and highly-dynamic tennis skills to successfully conduct competitive rallies with a high-speed tennis ball. However, reproducing such behaviors on humanoid robots is difficult, partially due to the lack of perfect humanoid action data or human kinematic motion data in tennis scenarios as reference. In this work, we propose LATENT, a system that Learns Athletic humanoid TEnnis skills from imperfect human motioN daTa. The imperfect human motion data consist only of motion fragments that capture the primitive skills used when playing tennis rather than precise and complete human-tennis motion sequences from real-world tennis matches, thereby significantly reducing the difficulty of data collection. Our key insight is that, despite being imperfect, such quasi-realistic data still provide priors about human primitive skills in tennis scenarios. With further correction and composition, we learn a humanoid policy that can consistently strike incoming balls under a wide range of conditions and return them to target locations, while preserving natural motion styles. We also propose a series of designs for robust sim-to-real transfer and deploy our policy on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot. Our method achieves surprising results in the real world and can stably sustain multi-shot rallies with human players. Project page: https://zzk273.github.io/LATENT/

</details>


### [69] [Altered Thoughts, Altered Actions: Probing Chain-of-Thought Vulnerabilities in VLA Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12717)
*Tuan Duong Trinh,Naveed Akhtar,Basim Azam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现VLA模型中的思维链推理存在安全漏洞：仅通过替换推理轨迹中的对象名称就能显著降低机器人任务成功率，而其他更复杂的攻击方式影响有限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉-语言-动作模型中思维链推理的安全性，特别是中间推理轨迹对动作解码器的实际依赖关系，以及是否仅通过破坏推理轨迹就能影响机器人的物理任务性能

Method: 设计了七种文本破坏方法，分为三个攻击层级（盲目噪声、机械语义、LLM自适应），在40个LIBERO桌面操作任务上对最先进的推理VLA模型进行测试，并使用非推理VLA模型作为跨架构对照

Result: 发现显著不对称性：替换对象名称使整体成功率下降8.3个百分点（目标条件任务下降19.3pp，个别任务下降45pp），而句子重排、空间方向反转、令牌噪声甚至70B参数LLM生成的错误计划影响可忽略（±4pp内）

Conclusion: 动作解码器依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量或顺序结构；内部推理轨迹是独特且隐蔽的威胁向量，不受输入验证防御措施影响；复杂的LLM攻击者表现不如简单的对象名称替换

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly adopt chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, generating a natural-language plan before decoding motor commands. This internal text channel between the reasoning module and the action decoder has received no adversarial scrutiny. We ask: which properties of this intermediate plan does the action decoder actually rely on, and can targeted corruption of the reasoning trace alone -- with all inputs left intact -- degrade a robot's physical task performance? We design a taxonomy of seven text corruptions organized into three attacker tiers (blind noise, mechanical-semantic, and LLM-adaptive) and apply them to a state-of-the-art reasoning VLA across 40 LIBERO tabletop manipulation tasks. Our results reveal a striking asymmetry: substituting object names in the reasoning trace reduces overall success rate by 8.3~percentage points (pp) -- reaching $-$19.3~pp on goal-conditioned tasks and $-$45~pp on individual tasks -- whereas sentence reordering, spatial-direction reversal, token noise, and even a 70B-parameter LLM crafting plausible-but-wrong plans all have negligible impact (within $\pm$4~pp). This asymmetry indicates that the action decoder depends on entity-reference integrity rather than reasoning quality or sequential structure. Notably, a sophisticated LLM-based attacker underperforms simple mechanical object-name substitution, because preserving plausibility inadvertently retains the entity-grounding structure the decoder needs. A cross-architecture control using a non-reasoning VLA confirms the vulnerability is exclusive to reasoning-augmented models, while instruction-level attacks degrade both architectures -- establishing that the internal reasoning trace is a distinct and stealthy threat vector invisible to input-validation defenses.

</details>


### [70] [Conflict Mitigation in Shared Environments using Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12736)
*Lukas Heuer,Yufei Zhu,Luigi Palmieri,Andrey Rudenko,Anna Mannucci,Sven Koenig,Martin Magnusson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FA-MAPF框架将不可控智能体的学习运动模式集成到集中式MAPF算法中，在多机器人系统中减少与动态不可控智能体的冲突


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人系统与动态不可控智能体共存的环境中，现有研究主要关注延迟情况下的MAPF解决方案完整性，而缺乏利用额外环境信息来提升解决方案质量的关注

Method: 提出Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding (FA-MAPF)框架，将学习到的不可控智能体运动模式集成到集中式MAPF算法中

Result: 在模拟不可控智能体的多样化基准地图和记录人类轨迹的真实世界地图上的评估显示，FA-MAPF相比最先进基线方法能减少最多55%的冲突，且不影响任务效率

Conclusion: FA-MAPF通过集成不可控智能体的学习运动模式，有效减少了多机器人系统与动态不可控智能体之间的冲突，提升了解决方案质量

Abstract: Deploying multi-robot systems in environments shared with dynamic and uncontrollable agents presents significant challenges, especially for large robot fleets. In such environments, individual robot operations can be delayed due to unforeseen conflicts with uncontrollable agents. While existing research primarily focuses on preserving the completeness of Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) solutions considering delays, there is limited emphasis on utilizing additional environmental information to enhance solution quality in the presence of other dynamic agents. To this end, we propose Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding (FA-MAPF), a novel framework that integrates learned motion patterns of uncontrollable agents into centralized MAPF algorithms. Our evaluation, conducted on a diverse set of benchmark maps with simulated uncontrollable agents and on a real-world map with recorded human trajectories, demonstrates the effectiveness of FA-MAPF compared to state-of-the-art baselines. The experimental results show that FA-MAPF can consistently reduce conflicts with uncontrollable agents, up to 55%, without compromising task efficiency.

</details>


### [71] [Easy-IIL: Reducing Human Operational Burden in Interactive Imitation Learning via Assistant Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12769)
*Chengjie Zhang,Chao Tang,Wenlong Dong,Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Easy-IIL框架利用现成的基于模型的模仿学习方法作为辅助专家，替代人类进行大部分数据收集，显著减少人类操作负担，同时保持交互式模仿学习性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互式模仿学习需要大量人类参与离线演示和在线交互，现有研究主要关注减少被动监控而非主动操作的人力负担。基于模型的模仿方法在低数据量时表现与端到端方法相当但需要更少演示，但数据增加时通常被超越

Method: 提出Easy-IIL框架，使用现成的基于模型模仿方法作为辅助专家替代人类主动操作。人类专家仅提供单次演示初始化辅助专家，并在任务接近失败的关键状态进行干预。框架通过保持离线和在线数据质量来维持IIL性能

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，Easy-IIL显著减少人类操作负担，同时保持与主流IIL基线相当的性能。用户研究进一步证实Easy-IIL降低了人类专家的主观工作负荷

Conclusion: Easy-IIL通过利用基于模型的模仿方法作为辅助专家，有效减少了交互式模仿学习中人类专家的主动操作负担，同时保持了学习性能，为降低IIL人力成本提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) typically relies on extensive human involvement for both offline demonstration and online interaction. Prior work primarily focuses on reducing human effort in passive monitoring rather than active operation. Interestingly, structured model-based imitation approaches achieve comparable performance with significantly fewer demonstrations than end-to-end imitation learning policies in the low-data regime. However, these methods are typically surpassed by end-to-end policies as the data increases. Leveraging this insight, we propose Easy-IIL, a framework that utilizes off-the-shelf model-based imitation methods as an assistant expert to replace active human operation for the majority of data collection. The human expert only provides a single demonstration to initialize the assistant expert and intervenes in critical states where the task is approaching failure. Furthermore, Easy-IIL can maintain IIL performance by preserving both offline and online data quality. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Easy-IIL significantly reduces human operational burden while maintaining performance comparable to mainstream IIL baselines. User studies further confirm that Easy-IIL reduces subjective workload on the human expert. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/easy-iil

</details>


### [72] [Motion-Specific Battery Health Assessment for Quadrotors Using High-Fidelity Battery Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12791)
*Joonhee Kim,Sanghyun Park,Donghyeong Kim,Eunseon Choi,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个端到端框架，用于评估四旋翼飞行器运动对电池健康的影响，通过高精度电池模型和实际飞行电流测量，分析不同飞行动作如何导致不同的电池退化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼飞行器的续航能力受限于电池性能，但现有的能量感知规划通常将电池视为简单的能量储存器，忽略了飞行运动引起的动态电流负载会加速电池退化的问题。

Method: 1) 设计宽范围电流传感模块，在真实飞行中捕捉运动特定的电流剖面，保留瞬态特征；2) 使用参考性能测试和基于退化耦合电化学模型的元启发式方法校准高保真电池模型；3) 通过在校准模型中模拟测量的飞行负载，系统分析不同飞行动作如何转化为退化模式（锂库存损失、活性材料损失）和内部副反应。

Result: 研究结果表明，即使两种飞行剖面消耗相同的平均能量，它们的瞬态负载结构也会驱动不同的退化途径，强调了需要平衡效率与电池退化的运动感知电池管理。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了在四旋翼飞行器电池管理中考虑运动特定电流负载的重要性，提出了一个能够量化不同飞行动作对电池退化影响的框架，为实现更智能的电池健康感知规划奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quadrotor endurance is ultimately limited by battery behavior, yet most energy aware planning treats the battery as a simple energy reservoir and overlooks how flight motions induce dynamic current loads that accelerate battery degradation. This work presents an end to end framework for motion aware battery health assessment in quadrotors. We first design a wide range current sensing module to capture motion specific current profiles during real flights, preserving transient features. In parallel, a high fidelity battery model is calibrated using reference performance tests and a metaheuristic based on a degradation coupled electrochemical model.By simulating measured flight loads in the calibrated model, we systematically resolve how different flight motions translate into degradation modes loss of lithium inventory and loss of active material as well as internal side reactions. The results demonstrate that even when two flight profiles consume the same average energy, their transient load structures can drive different degradation pathways, emphasizing the need for motion-aware battery management that balances efficiency with battery degradation.

</details>


### [73] [FLUX: Accelerating Cross-Embodiment Generative Navigation Policies via Rectified Flow and Static-to-Dynamic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12806)
*Zeying Gong,Yangyi Zhong,Yiyi Ding,Tianshuai Hu,Guoyang Zhao,Lingdong Kong,Rong Li,Jiadi You,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLUX：首个基于流的统一导航策略，通过线性化概率流实现高效推理，在动态社交导航基准DynBench上实现SOTA性能，并具备零样本仿真到现实迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主导航评估存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一的评估框架来覆盖从静态目标到达到动态社交穿越的完整技能谱系。需要开发能够处理多种导航任务的统一策略。

Method: 提出FLUX流式统一导航策略：1）通过线性化概率流，用直线轨迹替代迭代去噪，提升推理效率；2）采用静态到动态的课程学习，先建立几何先验，再通过强化学习在动态社交环境中精炼；3）利用随机动作分布捕捉恢复行为。

Result: FLUX在六项基本导航任务上实现最先进性能：相比先前基于流的方法提升47%的单步推理效率，相比基于扩散的方法提升29%。在轮式、四足和人形平台上实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，无需微调。

Conclusion: FLUX证明了基于流的统一导航策略的有效性，通过DynBench动态导航基准和静态到动态课程学习，实现了高效、鲁棒且可迁移的导航能力，为自主导航系统提供了全面评估框架。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation requires a broad spectrum of skills, from static goal-reaching to dynamic social traversal, yet evaluation remains fragmented across disparate protocols. We introduce DynBench, a dynamic navigation benchmark featuring physically valid crowd simulation. Combined with existing static protocols, it supports comprehensive evaluation across six fundamental navigation tasks. Within this framework, we propose FLUX, the first flow-based unified navigation policy. By linearizing probability flow, FLUX replaces iterative denoising with straight-line trajectories, improving per-step inference efficiency by 47% over prior flow-based methods and 29% over diffusion-based ones. Following a static-to-dynamic curriculum, FLUX initially establishes geometric priors and is subsequently refined through reinforcement learning in dynamic social environments. This regime not only strengthens socially-aware navigation but also enhances static task robustness by capturing recovery behaviors through stochastic action distributions. FLUX achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks and demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on wheeled, quadrupedal, and humanoid platforms without any fine-tuning.

</details>


### [74] [Reinforcement Learning for Elliptical Cylinder Motion Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12807)
*Pawel Marczewski,Paulina Superczynska,Jakub Bernat,Szymon Szczesny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用强化学习解决椭圆圆柱体在有限扭矩下的控制问题，并与基于能量整形的摆起控制器+LQR稳定器的经典方法进行比较


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限输入扭矩下的设备控制难题，特别是受无缆磁控设备启发的椭圆圆柱体运动控制问题，这类问题由于距离限制只能使用有限扭矩

Method: 采用强化学习方法，同时设计了经典的两阶段控制器作为基准：能量整形摆起控制器将系统驱动到目标平衡点附近，然后使用局部线性化模型的LQR进行稳定控制

Result: 学习控制是可行的，但对于质量增加或椭圆长短轴比差异较大的情况，向上位置稳定或半转旋转等不同场景变得非常困难

Conclusion: 强化学习能够解决椭圆圆柱体在有限扭矩下的控制问题，但某些特定情况（如大质量或极端椭圆形状）仍具有挑战性；经典的两阶段控制器为欠驱动系统提供了可解释的参考基准

Abstract: The control of devices with limited input always bring attention to solve by research due to its difficulty and non-trival solution. For instance, the inverted pendulum is benchmarking problem in control theory and machine learning. In this work, we are focused on the elliptical cylinder and its motion under limited torque. The inspiration of the problem is from untethered magnetic devices, which due to distance have to operate with limited input torque. In this work, the main goal is to define the control problem of elliptic cylinder with limited input torque and solve it by Reinforcement Learning. As a classical baseline, we evaluate a two-stage controller composed of an energy-shaping swing-up law and a local Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) stabilizer around the target equilibrium. The swing-up controller increases the system's mechanical energy to drive the state toward a neighborhood of the desired equilibrium, a linearization of the nonlinear model yields an LQR that regulates the angle and angular-rate states to the target orientation with bounded input. This swing-up + LQR policy is a strong, interpretable reference for underactuated system and serves a point of comparison to the learned policy under identical limits and parameters. The solution shows that the learning is possible however, the different cases like stabilization in upward position or rotating of half turn are very difficult for increasing mass or ellipses with a strongly unequal perimeter ratio.

</details>


### [75] [SmoothTurn: Learning to Turn Smoothly for Agile Navigation with Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12842)
*Zunzhi You,Haolan Guo,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoothTurn：一种用于四足机器人敏捷顺序局部导航的学习控制框架，通过顺序目标到达奖励、前瞻观察空间和自动目标课程，实现高速奔跑时的平滑转向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目标导航方法在顺序目标导航时无法预判即将到来的机动动作或保持切换目标时的动量，限制了四足机器人在消防救援、工业检查等紧急应用中的敏捷导航潜力

Method: 提出顺序局部导航任务表述，采用顺序目标到达奖励、包含未来目标前瞻窗口的扩展观察空间，以及基于目标到达性能逐步增加难度的自动目标课程

Result: 训练出的策略可直接部署在具有板载传感器和计算能力的真实四足机器人上，仿真和实际实验显示策略能执行跨目标的平滑转向，涌现出控制动量、提前面向未来目标和规划高效路径等行为

Conclusion: SmoothTurn框架成功解决了顺序目标导航中的平滑转向问题，使四足机器人能够充分发挥其敏捷潜力，适用于需要快速机动导航的实际应用场景

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots show great potential for valuable real-world applications such as fire rescue and industrial inspection. Such applications often require urgency and the ability to navigate agilely, which in turn demands the capability to change directions smoothly while running in high speed. Existing approaches for agile navigation typically learn a single-goal reaching policy by encouraging the robot to stay at the target position after reaching there. As a result, when the policy is used to reach sequential goals that require changing directions, it cannot anticipate upcoming maneuvers or maintain momentum across the switch of goals, thereby preventing the robot from fully exploiting its agility potential. In this work, we formulate the task as sequential local navigation, extending the single-goal-conditioned local navigation formulation in prior work. We then introduce SmoothTurn, a learning-based control framework that learns to turn smoothly while running rapidly for agile sequential local navigation. The framework adopts a novel sequential goal-reaching reward, an expanded observation space with a lookahead window for future goals, and an automatic goal curriculum that progressively expands the difficulty of sampled goal sequences based on the goal-reaching performance. The trained policy can be directly deployed on real quadrupedal robots with onboard sensors and computation. Both simulation and real-world empirical results show that SmoothTurn learns an agile locomotion policy that performs smooth turning across goals, with emergent behaviors such as controlling momentum when switching goals, facing towards the future goal in advance, and planning efficient paths. We have provided video demos of the learned motions in the supplementary materials. The source code and trained policies will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [76] [Consistent and Efficient MSCKF-based LiDAR-Inertial Odometry with Inferred Cluster-to-Plane Constraints for UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12904)
*Jinwen Zhu,Xudong Zhao,Fangcheng Zhu,Jun Hu,Shi Jin,Yinian Mao,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向无人机的高效紧耦合LiDAR-IMU里程计框架，通过滑动窗口共面约束和并行体素数据关联，在保持精度的同时显著提升了计算效率和一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-IMU里程计系统在无人机等SWaP受限平台上存在估计不一致性和计算瓶颈问题，需要一种既保持精度又能在资源受限设备上实时运行的方法

Method: 基于MSCKF框架构建滑动窗口共面约束，通过零空间投影消除特征参数依赖；引入并行体素数据关联和紧凑的聚类到平面测量模型，降低观测维度并加速更新过程

Result: 方法在一致性和效率方面优于现有SOTA方法，在退化场景中表现出更好的鲁棒性，内存使用最低（无地图特性），可在NVIDIA Jetson TX2等嵌入式平台上实时运行

Conclusion: 提出的紧耦合LIO框架为无人机导航提供了高效一致的解决方案，通过创新的约束处理和测量模型设计，在资源受限平台上实现了实时性能与精度的平衡

Abstract: Robust and accurate navigation is critical for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) especially for those with stringent Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints. However, most state-of-the-art (SOTA) LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (LIO) systems still suffer from estimation inconsistency and computational bottlenecks when deployed on such platforms. To address these issues, this paper proposes a consistent and efficient tightly-coupled LIO framework tailored for UAVs. Within the efficient Multi-State Constraint Kalman Filter (MSCKF) framework, we build coplanar constraints inferred from planar features observed across a sliding window. By applying null-space projection to sliding-window coplanar constraints, we eliminate the direct dependency on feature parameters in the state vector, thereby mitigating overconfidence and improving consistency. More importantly, to further boost the efficiency, we introduce a parallel voxel-based data association and a novel compact cluster-to-plane measurement model. This compact measurement model losslessly reduces observation dimensionality and significantly accelerating the update process. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms most state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches by providing a superior balance of consistency and efficiency. It exhibits improved robustness in degenerate scenarios, achieves the lowest memory usage via its map-free nature, and runs in real-time on resource-constrained embedded platforms (e.g., NVIDIA Jetson TX2).

</details>


### [77] [GoalSwarm: Multi-UAV Semantic Coordination for Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12908)
*MoniJesu Wonders James,Amir Atef Habel,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserokou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoalSwarm：用于零样本语义目标导航的完全去中心化多无人机框架，通过轻量级2D语义占据地图、零样本基础模型和贝叶斯价值地图实现开放词汇目标识别与多智能体协调


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人团队在未知环境中进行协作视觉语义导航面临两大挑战：重型感知模型在机载设备上的计算限制，以及去中心化多智能体协调的复杂性。现有方法难以实现鲁棒的开放词汇目标导航。

Method: 1. 构建共享轻量级2D自上而下语义占据地图：通过投影空中视点的深度观测，避免完整3D表示的计算负担；2. 集成零样本基础模型SAM3：实现开放词汇检测和像素级分割；3. 贝叶斯价值地图：融合多视点检测置信度，生成像素级目标相关性分布；4. 去中心化协调策略：结合语义边界提取、成本效用投标（使用测地线路径成本）和空间分离惩罚。

Result: 提出GoalSwarm框架，能够实现零样本语义目标导航，通过轻量级表示减少计算负担，利用贝叶斯融合提高目标识别可靠性，并通过去中心化协调策略最小化群体冗余探索。

Conclusion: GoalSwarm为多无人机团队提供了一种完全去中心化的解决方案，能够在未知环境中实现鲁棒的开放词汇语义目标导航，解决了计算约束和协调复杂性的双重挑战，为零样本多智能体语义导航开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Cooperative visual semantic navigation is a foundational capability for aerial robot teams operating in unknown environments. However, achieving robust open-vocabulary object-goal navigation remains challenging due to the computational constraints of deploying heavy perception models onboard and the complexity of decentralized multi-agent coordination. We present GoalSwarm, a fully decentralized multi-UAV framework for zero-shot semantic object-goal navigation. Each UAV collaboratively constructs a shared, lightweight 2D top-down semantic occupancy map by projecting depth observations from aerial vantage points, eliminating the computational burden of full 3D representations while preserving essential geometric and semantic structure. The core contributions of GoalSwarm are threefold: (1) integration of zero-shot foundation model -- SAM3 for open vocabulary detection and pixel-level segmentation, enabling open-vocabulary target identification without task-specific training; (2) a Bayesian Value Map that fuses multi-viewpoint detection confidences into a per-pixel goal-relevance distribution, enabling informed frontier scoring via Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) exploration; and (3) a decentralized coordination strategy combining semantic frontier extraction, cost-utility bidding with geodesic path costs, and spatial separation penalties to minimize redundant exploration across the swarm.

</details>


### [78] [MotionAnymesh: Physics-Grounded Articulation for Simulation-Ready Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12936)
*WenBo Xu,Liu Liu,Li Zhang,Dan Guo,RuoNan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionAnymesh：一个自动化零样本框架，将静态3D网格转换为可交互的铰接式数字孪生，通过物理先验消除运动学幻觉，确保无碰撞铰接


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本流水线缺乏物理基础，导致运动学幻觉和网格穿透问题，无法为具身AI和机器人仿真提供可靠的铰接式资产

Method: 1) 运动学感知部件分割模块：使用SP4D物理先验约束VLM推理；2) 几何-物理联合估计流水线：结合类型感知初始化和物理约束轨迹优化

Result: 在几何精度和动态物理可执行性方面显著优于现有基线，提供高度可靠的下游应用资产

Conclusion: MotionAnymesh成功解决了静态网格到仿真就绪数字孪生的转换问题，通过物理基础方法消除了运动学幻觉并保证无碰撞铰接

Abstract: Converting static 3D meshes into interactable articulated assets is crucial for embodied AI and robotic simulation. However, existing zero-shot pipelines struggle with complex assets due to a critical lack of physical grounding. Specifically, ungrounded Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently suffer from kinematic hallucinations, while unconstrained joint estimation inevitably leads to catastrophic mesh inter-penetration during physical simulation. To bridge this gap, we propose MotionAnymesh, an automated zero-shot framework that seamlessly transforms unstructured static meshes into simulation-ready digital twins. Our method features a kinematic-aware part segmentation module that grounds VLM reasoning with explicit SP4D physical priors, effectively eradicating kinematic hallucinations. Furthermore, we introduce a geometry-physics joint estimation pipeline that combines robust type-aware initialization with physics-constrained trajectory optimization to rigorously guarantee collision-free articulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MotionAnymesh significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both geometric precision and dynamic physical executability, providing highly reliable assets for downstream applications.

</details>


### [79] [RoboStream: Weaving Spatio-Temporal Reasoning with Memory in Vision-Language Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12939)
*Yuzhi Huang,Jie Wu,Weijue Bu,Ziyi Xiong,Gaoyang Jiang,Ye Li,Kangye Ji,Shuzhao Xie,Yue Huang,Chenglei Wu,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboStream：无需训练的机器人操作框架，通过时空融合令牌和因果时空图实现几何锚定和因果连续性，显著提升长时程操作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM规划器将每个步骤视为孤立的观察-动作映射，缺乏时空推理能力，无法持续跟踪几何关系和动作触发的状态转换，导致感知错误累积、被遮挡物体被遗忘、前提条件违反等问题

Method: 提出RoboStream框架：1）时空融合令牌（STF-Tokens）将视觉证据绑定到3D几何属性，实现持久对象锚定；2）因果时空图（CSTG）记录跨步骤的动作触发状态转换，保持因果连续性

Result: 在长时程RLBench上达到90.5%成功率，在真实世界积木搭建任务上达到44.4%，而SoFar和VoxPoser仅11.1%，证明时空推理和因果记忆对可靠长时程操作至关重要

Conclusion: 时空推理和因果记忆是实现可靠长时程机器人操作的关键缺失组件，RoboStream通过几何锚定和因果连续性跟踪，无需额外训练即可显著提升性能

Abstract: Enabling reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation is a crucial step toward open-world embodied intelligence. However, VLM-based planners treat each step as an isolated observation-to-action mapping, forcing them to reinfer scene geometry from raw pixels at every decision point while remaining unaware of how prior actions have reshaped the environment. Despite strong short-horizon performance, these systems lack the spatio-temporal reasoning required for persistent geometric anchoring and memory of action-triggered state transitions. Without persistent state tracking, perceptual errors accumulate across the execution horizon, temporarily occluded objects are catastrophically forgotten, and these compounding failures lead to precondition violations that cascade through subsequent steps. In contrast, humans maintain a persistent mental model that continuously tracks spatial relations and action consequences across interactions rather than reconstructing them at each instant. Inspired by this human capacity for causal spatio-temporal reasoning with persistent memory, we propose RoboStream, a training-free framework that achieves geometric anchoring through Spatio-Temporal Fusion Tokens (STF-Tokens), which bind visual evidence to 3D geometric attributes for persistent object grounding, and maintains causal continuity via a Causal Spatio-Temporal Graph (CSTG) that records action-triggered state transitions across steps. This design enables the planner to trace causal chains and preserve object permanence under occlusion without additional training or fine-tuning. RoboStream achieves 90.5% on long-horizon RLBench and 44.4% on challenging real-world block-building tasks, where both SoFar and VoxPoser score 11.1%, demonstrating that spatio-temporal reasoning and causal memory are critical missing components for reliable long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [80] [Coordinated Manipulation of Hybrid Deformable-Rigid Objects in Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12940)
*Anees Peringal,Anup Teejo Mathew,Panagiotis liatsis,Federico Renda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于准静态优化的混合可变形线性物体(hDLO)操作规划器，使用应变基Cosserat杆模型，在约束环境中实现刚柔混合组件的机器人操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调机器人操作可变形线性物体(DLOs)已被广泛研究，但在约束环境中处理同时包含可变形和刚性元件的混合组件仍然具有挑战性。现有方法难以在约束环境中实现混合组件的精确操作

Method: 采用准静态优化规划器，使用应变基Cosserat杆模型将刚体公式扩展到hDLO。利用可变形链节的柔顺性在约束中操作，通过可微分模型和解析梯度实现高效求解，包括逆运动静力学(IKS)和轨迹优化

Result: 方法在逆运动静力学问题上比有限差分基线快33倍；实验验证显示平均变形误差约3cm（可变形链节长度的5%）；与基于采样的可行性规划器相比，证明了所提方法的有效性和适用性

Conclusion: 提出的优化规划器在约束环境中有效解决了混合可变形线性物体的机器人操作问题，通过解析梯度实现了高效求解，为混合组件的精确操作提供了可行方案

Abstract: Coordinated robotic manipulation of deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as ropes and cables, has been widely studied; however, handling hybrid assemblies composed of both deformable and rigid elements in constrained environments remains challenging. This work presents a quasi-static optimization-based manipulation planner that employs a strain-based Cosserat rod model, extending rigid-body formulations to hybrid deformable linear objects (hDLO). The proposed planner exploits the compliance of deformable links to maneuver through constraints while achieving task-space objectives for the object that are unreachable with rigid tools. By leveraging a differentiable model with analytically derived gradients, the method achieves up to a 33x speedup over finite-difference baselines for inverse kinetostatic(IKS) problems. Furthermore, the subsequent trajectory optimization problem, warm-started using the IKS solution, is only practically realizable via analytical derivatives. The proposed algorithm is validated in simulation on various hDLO systems and experimentally on a three-link hDLO manipulated in a constrained environment using a dual-arm robotic system. Experimental results confirm the planner's accuracy, yielding an average deformation error of approximately 3 cm (5% of the deformable link length) between the desired and measured marker positions. Finally, the proposed optimal planner is compared against a sampling-based feasibility planner adapted to the strain-based formulation. The results demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of the proposed approach for robotic manipulation of hybrid assemblies in constrained environments.

</details>


### [81] [Language-Grounded Decoupled Action Representation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12967)
*Wuding Weng,Tongshu Wu,Liucheng Chen,Siyu Xie,Zheng Wang,Xing Xu,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaDA框架通过语言引导的解耦动作表示，使用三个可解释的动作基元（平移、旋转、夹持器控制）作为中间层，通过语义引导的对比学习增强动作泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人操作中高级视觉语言理解与低级动作控制之间的异构性挑战，现有方法在生成新颖或语义相关任务的鲁棒准确动作方面存在困难

Method: 提出语言引导的解耦动作表示框架，包含三个可解释动作基元作为中间层，采用语义引导的软标签对比学习目标对齐跨任务动作基元，并引入自适应加权策略平衡对比学习和模仿目标

Result: 在模拟基准（LIBERO和MimicGen）和真实世界演示中验证了LaDA的强性能，能够有效泛化到未见或相关任务

Conclusion: LaDA框架通过语言作为语义桥梁连接感知与控制，提供显式语义结构，增强动作泛化能力和运动一致性，为解决机器人操作中的异构性挑战提供了有效方案

Abstract: The heterogeneity between high-level vision-language understanding and low-level action control remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Although recent methods have advanced task-specific action alignment, they often struggle to generate robust and accurate actions for novel or semantically related tasks. To address this, we propose the Language-Grounded Decoupled Action Representation (LaDA) framework, which leverages natural language as a semantic bridge to connect perception and control. LaDA introduces a fine-grained intermediate layer of three interpretable action primitives--translation, rotation, and gripper control--providing explicit semantic structure for low-level actions. It further employs a semantic-guided soft-label contrastive learning objective to align similar action primitives across tasks, enhancing generalization and motion consistency. An adaptive weighting strategy, inspired by curriculum learning, dynamically balances contrastive and imitation objectives for stable and effective training. Extensive experiments on simulated benchmarks (LIBERO and MimicGen) and real-world demonstrations validate that LaDA achieves strong performance and generalizes effectively to unseen or related tasks.

</details>


### [82] [Route Fragmentation Based on Resource-centric Prioritisation for Efficient Multi-Robot Path Planning in Agricultural Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12994)
*James R. Heselden,Gautham P. Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 资源中心的多机器人路径规划算法在农业走廊环境中通过路线分段实现高吞吐量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业环境中存在大量空间密集的导航瓶颈，现有以智能体为中心的多机器人路径规划方法从智能体角度解决冲突，而非从竞争资源角度，限制了空间交错能力

Method: 提出了两种基于优先级的片段规划器变体，作为资源中心的MRPP算法，利用路线分段实现部分路线进展，限制基于二元的等待影响

Result: 在3.6公里拓扑地图的商业温室环境中进行终身模拟，与5种基线算法对比，片段规划器相比优先规划和基于优先级的搜索算法实现了显著的吞吐量提升，任务吞吐量达到同期最优任务吞吐量的95%

Conclusion: 对于走廊主导的农业环境中农业机器人的长期部署，资源中心的多机器人路径规划方法是实现高效操作规划的必要条件

Abstract: Agricultural environments present high proportions of spatially dense navigation bottlenecks for long-term navigation and operational planning of agricultural mobile robots. The existing agent-centric multi-robot path planning (MRPP) approaches resolve conflicts from the perspective of agents, rather than from the resources under contention. Further, the density of such contentions limits the capabilities of spatial interleaving, a concept that many planners rely on to achieve high throughput. In this work, two variants of the priority-based Fragment Planner (FP) are presented as resource-centric MRPP algorithms that leverage route fragmentation to enable partial route progression and limit the impact of binary-based waiting. These approaches are evaluated in lifelong simulation over a 3.6km topological map representing a commercial polytunnel environment. Their performances are contrasted against 5 baseline algorithms with varying robotic fleet sizes. The Fragment Planners achieved significant gains in throughput compared with Prioritised Planning (PP) and Priority-Based Search (PBS) algorithms. They further demonstrated a task throughput of 95% of the optimal task throughput over the same time period. This work shows that, for long-term deployment of agricultural robots in corridor-dominant agricultural environments, resource-centric MRPP approaches are a necessity for high-efficacy operational planning.

</details>


### [83] [From Passive Monitoring to Active Defence: Resilient Control of Manipulators Under Cyberattacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13003)
*Gabriele Gualandi,Alessandro V. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究冗余机械臂对隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击的防御，提出从被动监控到主动防御的架构，通过基于异常评分的主动控制级防御显著减少攻击引起的末端执行器偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理机器人系统容易受到虚假数据注入攻击，这些攻击能够破坏传感器信号同时规避基于残差的被动异常检测器（如卡方检验），导致末端执行器产生显著偏差而不触发警报。

Method: 构建包含反馈线性化机械臂、稳态卡尔曼滤波器和卡方异常检测器的闭环模型；在此基础上提出主动控制级防御，通过基于新型驱动投影、无需测量的状态预测器生成的异常评分的单调函数来衰减控制输入。

Result: 提出的防御设计提供了名义驱动损失的概率保证并保持闭环稳定性；从攻击者角度推导出计算一步最优隐蔽攻击的凸QCQP；在6自由度平面机械臂上的仿真表明，该防御显著减少了攻击引起的末端执行器偏差，同时在无攻击时保持了名义任务性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将冗余机械臂的防御架构从被动监控推进到主动防御，提出的控制级防御方法能有效应对隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击，在保证系统稳定性的同时显著降低攻击影响。

Abstract: Cyber-physical robotic systems are vulnerable to false data injection attacks (FDIAs), in which an adversary corrupts sensor signals while evading residual-based passive anomaly detectors such as the chi-squared test. Such stealthy attacks can induce substantial end-effector deviations without triggering alarms. This paper studies the resilience of redundant manipulators to stealthy FDIAs and advances the architecture from passive monitoring to active defence. We formulate a closed-loop model comprising a feedback-linearized manipulator, a steady-state Kalman filter, and a chi-squared-based anomaly detector. Building on this passive monitoring layer, we propose an active control-level defence that attenuates the control input through a monotone function of an anomaly score generated by a novel actuation-projected, measurement-free state predictor. The proposed design provides probabilistic guarantees on nominal actuation loss and preserves closed-loop stability. From the attacker perspective, we derive a convex QCQP for computing one-step optimal stealthy attacks. Simulations on a 6-DOF planar manipulator show that the proposed defence significantly reduces attack-induced end-effector deviation while preserving nominal task performance in the absence of attacks.

</details>


### [84] [SldprtNet: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for CAD Generation in Language-Driven 3D Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13098)
*Ruogu Li,Sikai Li,Yao Mu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SldprtNet是一个包含24.2万个工业零件的大规模多模态数据集，支持语义驱动的CAD建模、几何深度学习和多模态模型训练，提供3D模型、参数化文本、渲染图像和自然语言描述等多种对齐的数据模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏大规模、多模态对齐的工业零件数据集来支持语义驱动的CAD建模和跨模态学习。现有数据集在规模、模态多样性和数据对齐方面存在不足，限制了CAD生成和相关深度学习模型的发展。

Method: 1) 收集24.2万个工业零件3D模型(.step/.sldprt格式)；2) 开发编码器/解码器工具，支持13种CAD命令，实现3D模型与结构化文本表示的无损转换；3) 生成7个视角的合成渲染图像以减少输入token长度；4) 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B多模态模型生成自然语言描述；5) 人工验证并对齐所有模态数据；6) 在数据集子集上微调基线模型，比较图像+文本与纯文本输入的效果。

Result: 构建了包含24.2万个样本的SldprtNet数据集，每个样本包含：对齐的3D模型文件、参数化建模脚本、合成渲染图像和自然语言描述。实验结果表明，多模态输入(图像+文本)相比纯文本输入在CAD生成任务中表现更好，验证了多模态数据集的价值。

Conclusion: SldprtNet是一个全面、多模态对齐的工业零件数据集，支持语义驱动的CAD建模和跨模态学习。其真实工业零件选择、可扩展工具支持、多模态数据对齐以及模型复杂度多样性等特点，使其成为CAD生成和相关深度学习研究的重要资源，证明了多模态数据集在CAD领域的必要性和价值。

Abstract: We introduce SldprtNet, a large-scale dataset comprising over 242,000 industrial parts, designed for semantic-driven CAD modeling, geometric deep learning, and the training and fine-tuning of multimodal models for 3D design. The dataset provides 3D models in both .step and .sldprt formats to support diverse training and testing. To enable parametric modeling and facilitate dataset scalability, we developed supporting tools, an encoder and a decoder, which support 13 types of CAD commands and enable lossless transformation between 3D models and a structured text representation. Additionally, each sample is paired with a composite image created by merging seven rendered views from different viewpoints of the 3D model, effectively reducing input token length and accelerating inference. By combining this image with the parameterized text output from the encoder, we employ the lightweight multimodal language model Qwen2.5-VL-7B to generate a natural language description of each part's appearance and functionality. To ensure accuracy, we manually verified and aligned the generated descriptions, rendered images, and 3D models. These descriptions, along with the parameterized modeling scripts, rendered images, and 3D model files, are fully aligned to construct SldprtNet. To assess its effectiveness, we fine-tuned baseline models on a dataset subset, comparing image-plus-text inputs with text-only inputs. Results confirm the necessity and value of multimodal datasets for CAD generation. It features carefully selected real-world industrial parts, supporting tools for scalable dataset expansion, diverse modalities, and ensured diversity in model complexity and geometric features, making it a comprehensive multimodal dataset built for semantic-driven CAD modeling and cross-modal learning.

</details>


### [85] [Evaluating VLMs' Spatial Reasoning Over Robot Motion: A Step Towards Robot Planning with Motion Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13100)
*Wenxi Wu,Jingjing Zhang,Martim Brandão*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估四种最先进视觉语言模型在机器人运动空间推理能力，测试四种查询方法，发现Qwen2.5-VL零样本准确率达71.4%，微调后达75%，GPT-4o性能较低，分析准确性与计算成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解用户指令和物体空间关系对智能机器人系统至关重要。虽然基础模型已应用于任务规划，但尚不清楚它们在空间推理方面的能力程度，特别是执行用户偏好或运动约束（如与物体的期望距离、拓扑属性或运动风格偏好）的能力。

Method: 评估四种最先进的视觉语言模型在机器人运动空间推理能力，使用四种不同的查询方法。评估两种类型的运动偏好（物体接近度和路径风格），并分析准确性与计算成本（token数量）之间的权衡。

Result: 使用最高性能的查询方法，Qwen2.5-VL零样本准确率达到71.4%，在较小模型上微调后达到75%。GPT-4o导致较低性能。研究显示了准确性与计算成本之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 这项工作显示了视觉语言模型与机器人运动规划管道集成的潜在前景，表明VLMs在空间推理方面具有一定能力，但仍有改进空间，特别是在准确性和计算效率方面需要进一步优化。

Abstract: Understanding user instructions and object spatial relations in surrounding environments is crucial for intelligent robot systems to assist humans in various tasks. The natural language and spatial reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have the potential to enhance the generalization of robot planners on new tasks, objects, and motion specifications. While foundation models have been applied to task planning, it is still unclear the degree to which they have the capability of spatial reasoning required to enforce user preferences or constraints on motion, such as desired distances from objects, topological properties, or motion style preferences. In this paper, we evaluate the capability of four state-of-the-art VLMs at spatial reasoning over robot motion, using four different querying methods. Our results show that, with the highest-performing querying method, Qwen2.5-VL achieves 71.4% accuracy zero-shot and 75% on a smaller model after fine-tuning, and GPT-4o leads to lower performance. We evaluate two types of motion preferences (object-proximity and path-style), and we also analyze the trade-off between accuracy and computation cost in number of tokens. This work shows some promise in the potential of VLM integration with robot motion planning pipelines.

</details>


### [86] [A Feasibility-Enhanced Control Barrier Function Method for Multi-UAV Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13103)
*Qishen Zhong,Junlong Wu,Jian Yang,Guanwei Xiao,Junqi Wu,Zimeng Jiang,Pingan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可行性增强控制屏障函数(FECBF)框架，解决多无人机密集场景中CBF-QP可行性问题，通过引入符号一致性约束提高内部兼容性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集多无人机场景中，多个CBF约束之间的内部不兼容性会损害CBF二次规划(CBF-QP)的可行性，导致碰撞避免性能下降

Method: 分析CBF约束的内部兼容性，推导内部兼容性的充分条件，引入符号一致性约束缓解内部不兼容性，结合最坏情况估计和松弛变量构建分散式CBF-QP

Result: 仿真结果表明，与现有基线相比，该方法在密集场景中显著减少不可行性并提高碰撞避免性能，时变延迟下的仿真验证了鲁棒性，真实世界实验验证了实际适用性

Conclusion: 提出的FECBF框架通过解决CBF约束的内部兼容性问题，有效提高了多无人机密集场景中碰撞避免控制的可行性和性能

Abstract: This paper presents a feasibility-enhanced control barrier function (FECBF) framework for multi-UAV collision avoidance. In dense multi-UAV scenarios, the feasibility of the CBF quadratic program (CBF-QP) can be compromised due to internal incompatibility among multiple CBF constraints. To address this issue, we analyze the internal compatibility of CBF constraints and derive a sufficient condition for internal compatibility. Based on this condition, a sign-consistency constraint is introduced to mitigate internal incompatibility. The proposed constraint is incorporated into a decentralized CBF-QP formulation using worst-case estimates and slack variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces infeasibility and improves collision avoidance performance compared with existing baselines in dense scenarios. Additional simulations under varying time delays demonstrate the robustness of the proposed method. Real-world experiments validate the practical applicability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [87] [Panoramic Multimodal Semantic Occupancy Prediction for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13108)
*Guoqiang Zhao,Zhe Yang,Sheng Wu,Fei Teng,Mengfei Duan,Yuanfan Zheng,Kai Luo,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个四足机器人全景多模态占据数据集PanoMMOcc和对应感知框架VoxelHound，通过垂直抖动补偿和多模态提示融合提升复杂环境下的占据预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占据预测方法主要针对轮式自动驾驶设计，严重依赖RGB线索，在复杂环境中鲁棒性有限。四足机器人的全景视觉和腿部移动特性需要专门的数据集和感知方法

Method: 1) 创建PanoMMOcc数据集，包含四种感知模态；2) 提出VoxelHound框架，包含垂直抖动补偿模块(VJC)缓解身体俯仰滚转引起的视角扰动，以及多模态信息提示融合模块(MIPF)联合利用全景视觉和辅助模态增强体素占据预测

Result: 在PanoMMOcc基准测试中，VoxelHound达到最先进性能，mIoU提升4.16%。数据集和代码将公开以促进全景多模态3D感知研究

Conclusion: PanoMMOcc填补了四足机器人全景多模态占据数据集的空白，VoxelHound框架通过针对腿部移动和球形成像的专门设计，显著提升了复杂环境下的占据预测能力，为具身机器人系统的3D感知研究提供了重要基础

Abstract: Panoramic imagery provides holistic 360° visual coverage for perception in quadruped robots. However, existing occupancy prediction methods are mainly designed for wheeled autonomous driving and rely heavily on RGB cues, limiting their robustness in complex environments. To bridge this gap, (1) we present PanoMMOcc, the first real-world panoramic multimodal occupancy dataset for quadruped robots, featuring four sensing modalities across diverse scenes. (2) We propose a panoramic multimodal occupancy perception framework, VoxelHound, tailored for legged mobility and spherical imaging. Specifically, we design (i) a Vertical Jitter Compensation (VJC) module to mitigate severe viewpoint perturbations caused by body pitch and roll during mobility, enabling more consistent spatial reasoning, and (ii) an effective Multimodal Information Prompt Fusion (MIPF) module that jointly leverages panoramic visual cues and auxiliary modalities to enhance volumetric occupancy prediction. (3) We establish a benchmark based on PanoMMOcc and provide detailed data analysis to enable systematic evaluation of perception methods under challenging embodied scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VoxelHound achieves state-of-the-art performance on PanoMMOcc (+4.16%} in mIoU). The dataset and code will be publicly released to facilitate future research on panoramic multimodal 3D perception for embodied robotic systems at https://github.com/SXDR/PanoMMOcc, along with the calibration tools released at https://github.com/losehu/CameraLiDAR-Calib.

</details>


### [88] [DecoVLN: Decoupling Observation, Reasoning, and Correction for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13133)
*Zihao Xin,Wentong Li,Yixuan Jiang,Bin Wang,Runming Cong,Jie Qin,Shengjun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeCoVLN：用于长时程视觉语言导航的框架，通过自适应记忆优化和状态-动作对级纠错微调解决长期记忆构建和误差累积问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法面临两大挑战：1）构建有效的长期记忆库；2）克服误差累积问题。这些问题限制了智能体在复杂3D环境中执行长时程指令导航的能力。

Method: 提出DeCoVLN框架，包含两个核心技术：1）自适应细化机制，将长期记忆构建形式化为优化问题，通过迭代优化统一评分函数从历史候选池中选择关键帧，平衡语义相关性、视觉多样性和时间覆盖度；2）状态-动作对级纠错微调策略，利用状态间测地距离精确量化与专家轨迹的偏差，在可信区域内收集高质量数据并过滤低相关性污染数据。

Result: 大量实验证明了DeCoVLN的有效性，该框架已在真实世界环境中部署应用。

Conclusion: DeCoVLN通过创新的记忆优化和纠错机制，有效解决了视觉语言导航中的长期记忆构建和误差累积问题，提升了长时程导航的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow long-horizon instructions and navigate complex 3D environments. However, existing approaches face two major challenges: constructing an effective long-term memory bank and overcoming the compounding errors problem. To address these issues, we propose DecoVLN, an effective framework designed for robust streaming perception and closed-loop control in long-horizon navigation. First, we formulate long-term memory construction as an optimization problem and introduce adaptive refinement mechanism that selects frames from a historical candidate pool by iteratively optimizing a unified scoring function. This function jointly balances three key criteria: semantic relevance to the instruction, visual diversity from the selected memory, and temporal coverage of the historical trajectory. Second, to alleviate compounding errors, we introduce a state-action pair-level corrective finetuning strategy. By leveraging geodesic distance between states to precisely quantify deviation from the expert trajectory, the agent collects high-quality state-action pairs in the trusted region while filtering out the polluted data with low relevance. This improves both the efficiency and stability of error correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of DecoVLN, and we have deployed it in real-world environments.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Computing the Nonnegative Low-Rank Leading Eigenmatrix and its Applications to Markov Grids and Metzler Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12782)
*Nicolas Gillis,Carmela Scalone*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于微分系统时间积分的算法，计算线性矩阵值实算子最右特征对的非负低秩近似


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 计算最右特征对在两类应用中很重要：新型马尔可夫链（马尔可夫网格）和空间结构化系统（增长-扩散算子）；2. 非负性由Perron-Frobenius定理保证

Method: 基于时间积分适当微分系统的算法，解通过非负分解参数化，保持非负性

Result: 理论分析和计算实验表明算法相比标准方法更有效

Conclusion: 提出的算法能有效计算线性矩阵值实算子最右特征对的非负低秩近似，适用于马尔可夫网格和增长-扩散系统等应用

Abstract: We consider in this paper the problem of computing a nonnegative low-rank approximation of the rightmost eigenpair of a linear matrix-valued real operator. We propose an algorithm based on the time integration of a suitable differential system, whose solution is parametrized according to a nonnegative factorization. The conservation of the nonnegativity is theoretically motivated by the Perron-Frobenius theorem, while the computation of the rightmost eigenpair is motivated by two applications: (1) a new class of Markov chains, which we called Markov grids, whose transition matrices can be decomposed as the sum of Kronecker products, and (2) spatially structured systems in growth-diffusion operators arising for example in population and epidemic dynamics. Theoretical analysis and computational experiments show the effectiveness of the algorithm compared to standard approaches.

</details>


### [90] [Convergence of a Control Volume Finite Element scheme for a cross-diffusion system modeling ion transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12809)
*Arne Berrens,Robert Eymard*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于控制体积有限元（CVFE）的数值格式，用于近似求解在电场作用下化学物种在溶剂中的交叉扩散耦合系统，该格式在二维或三维空间的一般单纯网格上实现，满足熵不等式并收敛到连续问题的弱解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在电场作用下化学物种在溶剂中的交叉扩散耦合系统的数值模拟问题，需要开发一种能够在一般网格上保持物理特性（如熵不等式）的稳健数值格式，特别是在扩散系数可能退化的情况下。

Method: 采用控制体积有限元（CVFE）方法在二维或三维空间的一般单纯网格上离散化系统，数值未知量基于化学物种的化学势定义，设计格式以确保满足熵不等式。

Result: 所提出的数值格式满足熵不等式，从而为离散解提供了紧致性性质，并收敛到连续问题的弱解，在离子物种扩散退化的情形下通过数值算例验证了收敛性。

Conclusion: 该CVFE格式能够有效处理电场作用下化学物种交叉扩散耦合系统的数值模拟，即使在扩散退化的情况下也能保持物理一致性并保证收敛性，为这类复杂多物理场问题提供了可靠的数值工具。

Abstract: An approximation of a system coupling the cross-diffusion of chemical species within a solvent, subjected to an electric field, is obtained through a control volume finite element (CVFE) scheme on general simplicial meshes in two or three space dimensions. The discrete unknowns of the numerical scheme are derived from the chemical potential of the species. The scheme is designed in order to fulfill entropy inequalities, yielding compactness properties for the discrete solutions and convergence to a weak solution of the continuous problem. Numerical illustrations of the convergence properties are provided in situations where diffusion of ionic species degenerates.

</details>


### [91] [A Priori Adaptive Numerical Methods for Estimating Blow-up Times of Autonomous ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12957)
*Håkon Hoel,Johannes Vincent Meo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于辅助击中时间敏感性的自适应数值方法，用于估计自治ODE解的爆破时间，相比传统方法在计算效率上有优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发更有效的自适应数值方法来准确估计自治常微分方程解的爆破时间，解决传统方法在计算效率和精度方面的不足

Method: 基于辅助击中时间敏感性的自适应步长选择策略，通过理论误差分析验证方法的有效性，并与替代方法进行对比

Result: 理论证明了方法的误差收敛率，数值实验验证了自适应算法在计算效率上优于其他方法，实际性能良好

Conclusion: 提出的基于辅助击中时间敏感性的自适应方法能够有效估计ODE爆破时间，在计算效率和精度方面具有优势，理论和实验结果一致

Abstract: We present effective a priori adaptive numerical methods for estimating the blow-up time for solutions of autonomous ODEs. The novelty of our approach is to base our adaptive steps on the sensitivity of an auxiliary hitting time. We provide results on the theoretical error rates, and show that there is a benefit in terms of computational effort in choosing our adaptive algorithm over alternative approaches. Numerical experiments support our theoretical results and show how the methods perform in practice.

</details>


### [92] [A regularized method for quadratic optimization problems with finite-dimensional degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12959)
*C. G. Gebhardt,I. Romero*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种扰动正则化方法，用于近似具有有限维退化性的二次优化问题，通过正则化参数逼近原问题，并用有限元方法离散化，证明Γ收敛和极小化子收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有有限维退化性的二次优化问题，现有数值方法存在局限性。本文旨在推广Kaleem等人(2026)处理纯Neumann问题的方法，为Bochev & Lehoucq (2005)、Ivanov等人(2019)和Roccia等人(2024)的方法提供一种稀疏保持、数值高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出扰动正则化方法：首先通过小正参数正则化原问题，然后用有限元方法离散化。证明连续和离散泛函族Γ收敛到原问题泛函，且相应极小化子收敛。

Result: 建立了正则化问题的Γ收敛理论，证明了连续和离散极小化子的收敛性。该方法为处理退化二次优化问题提供了理论保证，并推广了纯Neumann问题的数值逼近方法。

Conclusion: 提出的扰动正则化方法能有效近似具有有限维退化性的二次优化问题，具有稀疏保持和数值高效的特点，为相关数值方法提供了理论框架和实用替代方案。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a perturbative regularization method to approximate quadratic optimization problems with finite-dimensional degeneracy. The original problem is first approximated by a regularized problem depending on a small positive parameter, and then discretized using the finite element method. The resulting families of continuous and discrete functionals $Γ$-converge to the functional of the original problem and the corresponding minimizers converge as well. Our method generalizes the approach proposed in Kaleem et al. (2026) for numerically approximating pure Neumann problems, which represents the cornerstone of a sparsity-preserving, numerically efficient alternative to the methods developed in Bochev and Lehoucq (2005), Ivanov et al. (2019) and Roccia et al. (2024). References: A. Kaleem, C. Gebhardt, and I. Romero. On the pure traction problem of linear elasticity: a regularized formulation and its robust approximation. arXiv preprint arXiv:2602.04359, 2026. P. Bochev and R. Lehoucq. On the finite element solution of the pure Neumann problem. SIAM Review, 47:50-66, 2005. M. Ivanov, I. Kremer, and M. Urev. Solving the pure Neumann problem by a finite element method. Numerical Analysis and Applications, 12:359-371, 2019. B. Roccia, C. Alturria, F. Mazzone, and C. Gebhardt. On the homogeneous torsion problem for heterogeneous and orthotropic cross-sections: theoretical and numerical aspects. Applied Numerical Mathematics, 201:579-607, 2024.

</details>


### [93] [Rapid Identification of Moving Contaminant Sources Through Physics-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12969)
*Marco Mattuschka,Jacopo Bonari,Max von Danwitz,Alexander Popp*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种结合稀疏测量与平流扩散模型的新算法，用于检测、定位和量化移动时变污染源，并配套开发了三维流场校准方法和数字孪生集成策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对恐怖主义或破坏活动中可能发生的危险物质释放场景，这些物质（如TICs）通常不可见且难以实时检测，需要快速可靠地评估大气污染源。

Method: 开发了一种新颖算法，将稀疏的CBRN传感器测量数据与平流扩散模型耦合，利用空间稀疏性作为先验信息来识别未知发生时间和轨迹的污染源；提出了基于风洞实验的三维流场模型校准方法；设计了与实时传感器数据在数字孪生环境中集成的策略。

Result: 在二维计算域中验证了方法的可行性，能够检测、定位和量化移动时变污染源；建立了三维流场校准方法以提高技术成熟度；提出了数字孪生环境下的实时决策支持框架。

Conclusion: 该研究为危险物质释放的应急响应提供了一套完整的解决方案，包括源项识别算法、流场校准方法和数字孪生集成策略，有望在紧急情况下实现预测性决策支持。

Abstract: In an act of sabotage or terrorism, hazardous material might be released deliberately into the atmosphere to threaten individuals, e.g., those operating critical infrastructure. Hazardous materials in such a scenario include toxic industrial chemicals (TICs), which are often invisible to the human eye, making it difficult to detect and respond to releases in a timely manner. This contribution considers the scenario of an airborne hazardous release requiring rapid and reliable assessment, with a chemical, biological, radiological, and nuclear (CBRN) sensor system providing scarce and local measurements. We present a novel algorithm that couples these data with an advection-diffusion model to detect, localize, and quantify a moving and time-varying contaminant source. Unlike many existing methods, the approach identifies sources with unknown occurrence time and trajectory by incorporating spatial sparsity as prior information. The feasibility of the approach is demonstrated in a two-dimensional computational domain. To further increase the technology readiness level, we additionally propose a calibration methodology for the required three-dimensional flow models based on wind tunnel experiments. Finally, a strategy for coupling the framework with real-time sensor data within a digital twin environment is outlined to enable predictive decision support in emergency scenarios.

</details>


### [94] [Adaptive FEM with optimal convergence rate for non-self-adjoint eigenvalue problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12973)
*Shixi Wang,Hai Bi,Yidu Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非自伴特征值问题的自适应有限元方法，提出了新的理论误差估计器和可计算误差估计器，证明了二者的等价性，并设计了达到最优收敛率的自适应算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非自伴特征值问题的自适应有限元方法，解决多重特征值和聚类特征值的误差估计问题，建立理论误差估计器与可计算误差估计器之间的联系。

Method: 1. 利用对应源问题的有限元解误差估计器，构建多重特征值和聚类特征值的理论误差估计器；2. 提出可计算的误差估计器并证明与理论估计器的等价性；3. 设计针对特征值聚类的自适应算法；4. 进行数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: 1. 建立了非自伴特征值问题自适应有限元方法的理论框架；2. 证明了理论误差估计器与可计算误差估计器的等价性；3. 所提出的自适应算法达到了最优收敛率；4. 数值实验支持了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 该研究为非自伴特征值问题的自适应有限元方法提供了完整的理论分析和实用算法，解决了多重特征值和聚类特征值的误差估计问题，证明了自适应算法的最优收敛性。

Abstract: In this paper, we first discuss the optimal convergence of the adaptive finite element methods for non-self-adjoint eigenvalue problems. We present new theoretical error estimators and computable error estimators for multiple and clustered eigenvalues with the help of the error estimators of finite element solutions for the corresponding source problems, and prove the equivalence between these two estimators. We propose an adaptive algorithm for the eigenvalue cluster and demonstrate that it achieves the optimal convergence rate.We also provide numerical experiments to support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [95] [Reduced rank extrapolation for multi-term Sylvester equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12979)
*Peter Benner,Pascal den Boef,Patrick Kürschner,Xiaobo Liu,Jens Saak*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了使用降秩外推法(RRE)加速多项Sylvester方程的稳态迭代，提供了理论和实现方法，包括大规模问题的低秩近似策略，实验显示RRE能显著提升收敛速度并减少存储和计算时间消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项Sylvester方程的稳态迭代方法收敛速度较慢，需要消耗大量存储和计算时间。研究者希望找到有效的加速技术来改善这一状况，特别是针对大规模问题。

Method: 采用降秩外推法(RRE)来加速多项Sylvester方程的稳态迭代。针对小规模问题提供理论收敛分析和实现；针对大规模问题，提出使用低秩矩阵近似的非精确非稳态迭代方法。

Result: 数值实验表明RRE加速具有显著潜力，通常能带来收敛速度的大幅提升，从而有效减少存储空间和计算时间的消耗。

Conclusion: 降秩外推法(RRE)是加速多项Sylvester方程稳态迭代的有效方法，特别适用于大规模问题，通过低秩近似策略能够实现计算效率的显著提升。

Abstract: We investigate the acceleration of stationary iterations for multi-term Sylvester equation by means of reduced rank extrapolation (RRE). Theoretical convergence results and implementations are provided for both small and large-scale problems. For the large-scale problems, an inexact non-stationary iteration is discussed, which makes use of low-rank matrix approximations. Numerical experiments illustrate the potential of the RRE acceleration which often leads to a substantial gain in convergence speed and therefore reducing the consumption of storage and computing time.

</details>


### [96] [Discretization of the Burgers' equation as a port-Hamiltonian system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12992)
*Lorenzo Agostini,Michel Fournié,Ghislain Haine*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了无粘性和粘性Burgers方程的端口哈密顿公式，采用有限元方法推导有限维端口哈密顿系统，分析了数值稳定性条件，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无粘性Burgers方程的数值模拟在间断附近常出现虚假振荡问题，通过引入粘性项可缓解此问题。需要建立既能处理对流效应又能处理耗散效应的统一数学框架。

Method: 为无粘性和粘性Burgers方程提出端口哈密顿公式，在该框架内自然处理边界控制和观测。应用专用有限元方法推导有限维端口哈密顿系统，分析时间步长、空间分辨率和粘性系数之间的数值稳定性关系。

Result: 成功建立了Burgers方程的端口哈密顿表示，推导出有限维离散系统，确定了保证数值稳定性的参数关系。数值实验验证了该方法在抑制虚假振荡方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 端口哈密顿框架为Burgers方程提供了统一的数学表示，既能处理对流效应又能处理耗散效应，有限元离散方法结合稳定性分析确保了数值模拟的可靠性，为偏微分方程的数值求解提供了新思路。

Abstract: The numerical simulation of the inviscid Burgers' equation is often hindered by spurious oscillations near discontinuities. To mitigate this issue, a viscous term can be introduced, leading to the viscous Burgers' equation. In this work, port-Hamiltonian formulations for both the inviscid and the viscous Burgers' equations are proposed, enabling a representation that incorporates both convective and dissipative effects. Boundary control and observation are naturally handled within this framework. Applying a dedicated finite element method, a finite-dimensional port-Hamiltonian system is derived. The relationship between time step, spatial resolution, and viscosity required to achieve numerical stability is analyzed. Numerical experiments validate the effectiveness of the approach.

</details>


### [97] [Augmented Lagrangian preconditioners for fictitious domain formulations of elliptic interface problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12993)
*Michele Benzi,Marco Feder,Luca Heltai,Federica Mugnaioni*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种用于椭圆界面问题（带跳跃系数）有限元离散线性系统的新型增广拉格朗日预处理器，基于虚拟域分布式拉格朗日乘子法，旨在改善FGMRES在大系数跳跃时的收敛性，并提供计算成本更低的块三角变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆界面问题中的大系数跳跃会导致传统求解器收敛缓慢，需要开发鲁棒的预处理器来加速FGMRES方法的收敛，同时降低计算成本。

Method: 基于虚拟域分布式拉格朗日乘子法（Fictitious Domain with Distributed Lagrange Multipliers）构建增广拉格朗日预处理器，包含理想AL预处理器及其计算成本更低的块三角变体，分析特征值聚类特性。

Result: 证明了理想AL预处理器的特征值聚类性质，分析了修改变体在参数和跳跃大小影响下的谱极限行为，数值实验显示网格无关的迭代次数和对大系数跳跃的鲁棒性，修改方法显著减少了计算时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的增广拉格朗日预处理器能有效处理椭圆界面问题中的大系数跳跃，块三角变体在保持鲁棒性的同时显著降低计算成本，为复杂几何和大系数对比问题提供了高效求解方案。

Abstract: We present a novel augmented Lagrangian (AL) preconditioner for the solution of linear systems arising from finite element discretizations of elliptic interface problems with jump coefficients. The method is based on the Fictitious Domain with Distributed Lagrange Multipliers formulation and it is designed to improve the convergence of the Flexible Generalized Minimal Residual (FGMRES) method in the presence of large coefficient jumps. To reduce the computational cost, we also introduce a cheaper block-triangular variant of the preconditioner. We prove eigenvalue clustering for the ideal AL preconditioner and study the limiting behavior of the spectrum for the modified variant in terms of parameters and the size of the jumps. Numerical experiments on different immersed geometries confirm mesh-independent iteration counts and robustness over large coefficient jumps, with substantial reductions in wall-clock time for the modified approach.

</details>


### [98] [Embedded Trefftz DG method for the Helmholtz equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13034)
*Paul Stocker,Igor Voulis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种嵌入式Trefftz间断伽辽金方法求解亥姆霍兹方程，通过局部约束实现Trefftz特性，使用对称内罚DG格式进行全局耦合，基于T-强制性和Schatz对偶技术证明波数显式稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Trefftz方法需要显式构造Trefftz基函数，计算复杂且不灵活。本文旨在开发一种避免显式构造Trefftz基函数的方法，同时保持Trefftz方法的优势。

Method: 从多项式DG空间出发，通过局部约束强制Trefftz特性；使用对称内罚DG双线性形式进行全局耦合；采用T-强制性论证结合Schatz型对偶技术分析稳定性。

Result: 在显式网格分辨率条件下，获得了波数显式的稳定性、拟最优性和标准DG范数下的收敛性估计。

Conclusion: 提出的嵌入式Trefftz DG方法避免了显式Trefftz基函数构造，同时保持了Trefftz方法的优势，为亥姆霍兹方程提供了有效的数值求解框架。

Abstract: We study an embedded Trefftz discontinuous Galerkin method for the Helmholtz equation. The method starts from a polynomial DG space and enforces the Trefftz property through local constraints, avoiding an explicit construction of Trefftz basis functions. For the global coupling we use a simple symmetric interior penalty DG bilinear form. Since the resulting formulation is not coercive, stability is proved by a $T$-coercivity argument combined with a Schatz-type duality technique. This yields wavenumber-explicit stability, quasi-optimality, and convergence estimates in standard DG norms under an explicit mesh resolution condition.

</details>


### [99] [PhaseJumps: fast computation of zeros from planar grid samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13158)
*Antti Haimi,Günther Koliander,José Luis Romero*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: PhaseJumps算法通过比较复相位变化和局部振荡，从有限网格样本计算复函数零点，适用于非解析函数，并能计算零点周围的相位缠绕方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种有效方法计算短时傅里叶变换的零点，克服以往对解析变换的限制，使信号处理中的最新见解更广泛适用。

Method: 基于比较网格相邻点间的复相位变化和局部振荡，算法能够处理可能非解析的输入函数，并计算零点周围的相位缠绕方向。

Result: 在网格间距为δ的情况下，算法以O(log²(1/δ)δ)的失败概率，在Wasserstein度量下实现√δ精度的零点计算，数值实验表明性能更优。

Conclusion: PhaseJumps为计算一般分析窗下模拟信号短时傅里叶变换的零点提供了首个有效方法，其失败情况对加性噪声具有鲁棒性（平滑分析）。

Abstract: We consider complex-valued functions on the complex plane and the task of computing their zeros from samples taken along a finite grid. We introduce PhaseJumps, an algorithm based on comparing changes in the complex phase and local oscillations among grid neighboring points. The algorithm is applicable to possibly non-analytic input functions, and also computes the direction of phase winding around zeros.
  PhaseJumps provides a first effective means to compute the zeros of the short-time Fourier transform of an analog signal with respect to a general analyzing window, and makes certain recent signal processing insights more widely applicable, overcoming previous constraints to analytic transformations.
