We study the performance of (a variant of) PhaseJumps under a stochastic input model motivated by signal processing applications and show that the input instances that may cause the algorithm to fail are fragile, in the sense that they are regularized by additive noise (smoothed analysis). Precisely, given samples of a function on a grid with spacing $δ$, we show that our algorithm computes zeros with accuracy $\sqrtδ$ in the Wasserstein metric with failure probability $O\big(\log^2(\tfrac{1}δ) δ\big)$, while numerical experiments suggests even better performance.

</details>


### [100] [Rigorous foundations of adaptive mode tracking in single-parametric Hermitian eigenvalue problems: existence theorems, error indicators, and application to SAFE dispersion analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13193)
*Dong Xiao,Zahra Sharif-Khodaei,M. H. Aliabadi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出用于SAFE方法中导波模态跟踪的严格理论框架，解决模态接近简并区域的跟踪难题，开发自适应波数采样算法提高计算效率和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAFE方法广泛用于计算任意截面波导的导波频散曲线，但在连续波数步长中准确跟踪模态仍然具有挑战性，特别是在模态接近简并区域，特征值几乎简并且特征向量变化剧烈。

Method: 建立单参数厄米特特征值问题的模态跟踪理论框架，推导特征向量导数的显式表达式，揭示其对特征值间隙的逆依赖关系。提出自适应波数采样算法，当MAC分离度低于容差时自动细化离散化，使用新的误差指标量化跟踪置信度。

Result: 验证了对称和非对称层合板、L形杆和钢管中的鲁棒跟踪，在接近简并区域使用比均匀采样或基于连续方法更少的点。与开源代码SAFEDC和Dispersion Calculator的比较证实了准确性和效率。

Conclusion: 该框架为可靠的频散曲线计算提供了理论保证和实用工具，解决了SAFE方法中模态跟踪的关键挑战，特别是在模态接近简并区域。

Abstract: The Semi-Analytical Finite Element (SAFE) method is widely used for computing guided wave dispersion curves in waveguides of arbitrary cross-section. Accurate mode tracking across consecutive wavenumber steps remains challenging, particularly in mode veering regions where eigenvalues become nearly degenerate and eigenvectors vary rapidly. This work establishes a rigorous theoretical framework for mode tracking in single-parameter Hermitian eigenvalue problems arising from SAFE formulations. We derive an explicit expression for the eigenvector derivative, revealing its inverse dependence on the eigenvalue gap, and prove that for any wavenumber and mode there exists a sufficiently small step ensuring unambiguous identification via the Modal Assurance Criterion. For symmetry-protected crossings, the Wigner-von Neumann non-crossing rule guarantees bounded eigenvector derivatives and reliable tracking even with coarse sampling. For continuous symmetries leading to degenerate subspaces, we introduce a rotation-invariant subspace MAC that treats each degenerate pair as a single entity. Based on these insights, we propose an adaptive wavenumber sampling algorithm that automatically refines the discretization where the MAC separation falls below a tolerance, using a novel error indicator to quantify tracking confidence. Validation on symmetric and unsymmetric laminates, an L-shaped bar, and a steel pipe demonstrates robust tracking in veering regions with substantially fewer points than uniform sampling or continuation-based approaches, while comparisons with open-source codes SAFEDC and Dispersion Calculator confirm accuracy and efficiency. The framework provides both theoretical guarantees and practical tools for reliable dispersion curve computation.

</details>

### [101] [Modeling diesel output particulate matter as the Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12356)
*Maxwell Bolt,Alex Alberts,Akash S. Desai,Peter Meckl,Ilias Bilionis*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出基于Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的柴油机颗粒物预测模型，通过参数化SDE方程适应发动机状态变化，实现低成本、带不确定性校准的PM排放预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柴油机颗粒物(PM)排放预测具有挑战性，随着排放标准趋严，制造商需要可靠且计算成本低的预测方法来量化生产发动机的PM排放。商用传感器精度不足，传统物理模型复杂，数据驱动方法在瞬态工况下因PM生成的随机性而表现不佳。

Method: 引入基于Ornstein-Uhlenbeck(OU)过程的PM预测模型。OU过程是一种均值回归的随机过程，常用于金融建模。通过将SDE方程的参数化为发动机状态的函数，并使用最大似然估计进行拟合，使模型能够适应发动机状态变化。

Result: 在合成示例中验证了模型学习时变参数化OU过程的能力。使用覆盖发动机运行空间的真实实验数据训练模型，并在EPA监管的驾驶循环上进行测试。结果表明，对于大多数驾驶循环，该方法能够准确预测PM随时间累积输出。

Conclusion: 基于Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程的PM预测模型提供了一种计算成本低、具有不确定性校准的PM排放预测方法，能够有效应对柴油机PM生成的随机性和瞬态工况挑战，为发动机制造商提供了可靠的PM量化工具。

Abstract: Diesel engine particulate matter (PM) is one of the most challenging emission constituents to predict. As engines become cleaner and emissions levels drop, manufacturers need reliable methods to quantify the PM generated by production engines. Due to the inaccuracy of commercial-grade sensors, they turn to predictive models to accurately estimate PM. In practice, this requires a computationally inexpensive model that provides PM estimates with calibrated uncertainty. Complex, multiscale physics make mechanistic models intractable and traditional data-driven methods struggle in transient drive cycles due to the stochastic nature of PM generation. Leveraging recent innovations in PM measurement technology, we introduce a novel PM model based on the Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) process. The OU process is a mean-reverting stochastic process commonly used in financial modeling, now being explored for engineering applications, and can be described as a stochastic differential equation (SDE). We modify the OU process by parameterizing the terms of the SDE as functions of the engine state, which are then fit with a maximum likelihood estimate. In a synthetic example, we verify the ability of our model to learn a time-varying, parametrized OU process. We then train the model using real experimental data designed to dynamically cover the engine operating space and test the trained model on EPA-regulated drive cycles. For most drive cycles, we find the method accurately predicts cumulative output of PM across time.

</details>


### [102] [Spatio-temporal evolution of surface temperature trends in Ghana (1983-2021): a multi-station approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12394)
*John Bagiliko,David Stern,Denis Ndanguza*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 对加纳22个气象站1983-2021年温度趋势的详细时空分析，揭示最小温度比最大温度上升更快，导致昼夜温差缩小，对农业和公共健康构成威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然现有研究为理解加纳气候提供了基础，但仍有通过站点级分析增强这一领域的空间。高分辨率分析能捕捉局部气候细微差异，补充现有研究。

Method: 使用22个气象站1983-2021年的日最高和最低温度观测数据，按照WMO标准进行质量控制、均一性测试和均一化处理（以AgERA5再分析数据为参考）。使用改进的Mann-Kendall检验（处理自相关效应）和Sen斜率估计器确定趋势的显著性和幅度。

Result: 加纳的温度趋势高度局部化和季节性。关键发现是不对称变暖：最小温度比最大温度上升更快，导致昼夜温差缩小。这种变化削弱了夜间冷却，对农业稳定性和公共健康构成重大威胁。

Conclusion: 研究强调迫切需要针对特定地点和季节的气候监测，以制定定制化适应策略。建议建立以造林和向绿色能源转型为重点的稳健政策框架来缓解这些影响。

Abstract: Surface temperature is a fundamental Essential Climate Variable, serving as a primary indicator of climate change and exerting a profound influence on ecosystems, agriculture, and human livelihoods. Although existing research provides a foundation for understanding the climate of Ghana, there remains an opportunity to enhance this landscape with granular station-level analysis. Such high-resolution analysis complements existing studies by capturing localised climatic nuances. This study conducts a detailed spatio-temporal analysis of temperature trends across 22 meteorological stations from 1983 to 2021. Using daily maximum (Tmax) and minimum (Tmin) observations, data were subjected to quality control, homogeneity testing, and homogenisation according to World Meteorological Organisation (WMO) standards, using AgERA5 reanalysis as a reference.The significance and magnitude of trends were determined using the Modified Mann-Kendall test, which is robust in handling potential effects of autocorrelation, and Sen's slope estimator. Results revealed that temperature trends in Ghana are highly localised and seasonal, highlighting the necessity for more studies of this nature. A critical finding is the asymmetric warming across the country, with minimum temperatures rising at an accelerated rate compared to maximum temperatures. This narrowing of the diurnal temperature range poses significant threats to agricultural stability and public health because nocturnal cooling is diminished. These findings underscore the urgent need for site-specific, seasonal climate monitoring to inform customised adaptation strategies. To mitigate these impacts, the study recommends a robust policy framework focusing on afforestation and the transition to green energy.

</details>


### [103] [Multivariate lattice deformation: A spatially explicit framework for assessing crop rotation impacts on soil nutrient dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12881)
*Marco Mandap*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出多变量晶格模型，将土壤视为4D张量（空间、时间、N、P、K通道），量化作物轮作对土壤养分的空间异质性影响，识别风险区域并指导精准管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法评估作物轮作对土壤养分影响时，通常采用田间平均或单养分分析，忽略了空间异质性和多变量相互作用，无法全面反映轮作对土壤养分的真实影响。

Method: 提出多变量晶格模型，将土壤视为4D张量（空间、时间、N、P、K通道）；作物轮作表示为力向量，土壤缓冲能力（"刚度"）随质地空间变化；引入核平滑处理侧向养分移动；使用N-P-K空间中的欧几里得距离量化累积影响，通过Cramer-von Mises置换检验评估显著性。

Result: 模拟三年玉米-大豆-小麦轮作显示：一个周期后平均应力为0.63，沙质区域最大达0.91；磷耗竭（17.9%）超过氮（10.8%），在19%的网格中主导应力；连续玉米种植使平均应力增加41%；Cramer-von Mises检验检测到显著偏差（p≤0.002），Moran's I（0.29-0.30）确认空间自相关。

Conclusion: 该框架能够识别风险区域并指导特定地点管理，将地统计学与机理性作物模型相结合，为精准农业提供更全面的土壤养分管理工具。

Abstract: Crop rotation impacts on soil nutrients are typically assessed using field-averaged or single-nutrient analyses that ignore spatial heterogeneity and multivariate interactions. We propose a multivariate lattice model treating soil as a 4D tensor (space, time, and N, P, K channels). Crop rotations are represented as force vectors, with soil buffering capacity ("stiffness") varying spatially with texture. Lateral nutrient movement is introduced via kernel smoothing. Cumulative impact is quantified by Euclidean distance in N-P-K space, with significance assessed via Cramer-von Mises permutation tests. Simulating a three-year corn-soybean-wheat rotation on a 20 x 20 heterogeneous grid shows mean stress of 0.63 after one cycle, with maximum 0.91 in sandy areas. Phosphorus depletion (17.9%) exceeds nitrogen (10.8%), dominating stress in 19% of cells - obscured by single-nutrient analyses. Continuous corn increases mean stress by 41%. Cramer-von Mises tests detect significant deviation (p <= 0.002), and Moran's I (0.29-0.30) confirms spatial autocorrelation. Our framework identifies risk zones and guides site-specific management, bridging geostatistics with mechanistic crop models.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Periodic Points of Power Maps in Finite Matrix Groups and Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12295)
*Saikat Panja*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究有限域上矩阵代数和线性群中幂映射x↦x^L的周期点，计算当q→∞且v_L(q-1)=c时，周期点比例在M_ℓ(q)、GL_ℓ(q)、Sp_{2ℓ}(q)和U_ℓ(q)中的极限值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上矩阵代数和线性群中幂映射的动力学性质，特别是当域的大小趋于无穷大时，周期点的渐近比例。这有助于理解代数结构中的迭代映射行为

Method: 分析幂映射x↦x^L在M_n(q)和GL_n(q)中的周期点，其中L是素数且L|q-1。通过计算当q→∞且v_L(q-1)=c时，周期点集合大小的极限比例。特别关注正则半单元素在确定极限值中的作用

Result: 确定了M_n(q)和GL_n(q)中幂映射的周期点，计算了M_ℓ(q)、GL_ℓ(q)、Sp_{2ℓ}(q)和U_ℓ(q)中周期点比例的极限值。发现Sp_{2ℓ}(q)和U_ℓ(q)的极限值相同，且正则半单元素在确定极限值中起关键作用

Conclusion: 正则半单元素在有限域上矩阵代数和线性群的幂映射动力学中扮演核心角色，决定了当域大小趋于无穷大时期限点比例的渐近行为。不同群类型的极限值表现出有趣的相似性

Abstract: Consider the power map $x\mapsto x^L$ for a prime $L\neq 2$ such that $L|q-1$ where $q$ is a power of a prime. We determine the periodic points under this map for $\operatorname{M}_n(q)$, the algebra of $n\times n $ matrices over a finite field of order $q$, and also for the group $\operatorname{GL}_n(q)=\operatorname{M}_n(q)^\times$. We compute the limit $ \lim\limits_{\substack{q\longrightarrow \infty\\v_L(q-1)=c}}\dfrac{\left|\operatorname{Per}(x^L,\operatorname{M}_\ell(q))\right|}{|\operatorname{M}_\ell(q)|}$ and consequently $\lim\limits_{\substack{q\longrightarrow \infty
  v_L(q-1)=c}}\dfrac{\left|\operatorname{Per}(x^L,\operatorname{GL}_\ell(q))\right|}{|\operatorname{GL}_\ell(q)|}$, where $v_L$ denotes the $L$-adic valuation. We also compute the quantity $\lim\limits_{\substack{q\longrightarrow \infty
  v_L(q-1)=c}}\dfrac{\left|\operatorname{Per}(x^L,\operatorname{Sp}_{2\ell}(q))\right|}{|\operatorname{Sp}_{2\ell}(q)|}$ and $\lim\limits_{\substack{q\longrightarrow \infty
  v_L(q-1)=c}}\dfrac{\left|\operatorname{Per}(x^L,\operatorname{U}_\ell(q))\right|}{|\operatorname{U}_\ell(q)|}$; turns out these two limiting values are same.
  In all the cases, it turns out that the regular semisimple elements play the role in determining the limiting values.

</details>


### [105] [Higher Fitting ideals and the structure of anticyclotomic Shafarevich-Tate groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12357)
*Enrico Da Ronche,Matteo Longo,Stefano Vigni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有理椭圆曲线在虚二次域反循环Zp-扩张上的Iwasawa主猜想的精细化版本，描述了Selmer群和Shafarevich-Tate群的Pontryagin对偶的高阶Fitting理想，并应用于这些群的结构研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有理椭圆曲线（及更一般的Galois表示）在虚二次域反循环Zp-扩张上的精细化Iwasawa主猜想，包括确定和不定两种情况。旨在理解Selmer群和Shafarevich-Tate群在反循环扩张中的结构特性。

Method: 在温和的算术假设下，使用Bertolini和Darmon引入的二部Euler系统来描述反循环Zp-扩张上Selmer群和Shafarevich-Tate群的Pontryagin对偶的所有高阶Fitting理想。将Fitting理想与Euler系统联系起来。

Result: 获得了反循环Selmer群和Shafarevich-Tate群的Pontryagin对偶的高阶Fitting理想的完整描述，这些描述用二部Euler系统表示。提供了关于这些群结构的新结果，深化了对Iwasawa理论中这些重要不变量行为的理解。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了反循环Zp-扩张上椭圆曲线Selmer群和Shafarevich-Tate群的Fitting理想与二部Euler系统之间的精确对应关系，为精细化Iwasawa主猜想提供了新视角，并获得了这些算术群结构的重要进展。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime number. We investigate a refined version of the Iwasawa main conjectures for rational elliptic curves (and more general Galois representations) over anticyclotomic $\mathbb Z_p$-extensions of imaginary quadratic fields, both in the definite and in the indefinite settings. In order to do this, we describe (under mild arithmetic assumptions) all the higher Fitting ideals of Pontryagin duals of Selmer and Shafarevich-Tate groups over anticyclotomic $\mathbb Z_p$-extensions in terms of the bipartite Euler systems introduced by Bertolini and Darmon. As an application of our work on Fitting ideals, we offer new results on the structure of (Pontryagin duals of) anticyclotomic Selmer and Shafarevich-Tate groups of elliptic curves.

</details>


### [106] [Proof of a Conjecture on Overcolored Partition Restricted by Parity of the Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12401)
*Imdadul Hussain,Suparno Ghoshal,Arijit Jana*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对Thejitha和Fathima提出的关于过着色配分函数$\bar{a}_{r,s}(n)$的模2幂次同余猜想给出了初等证明


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thejitha和Fathima引入了过着色配分函数$\bar{a}_{r,s}(n)$，该函数枚举了偶部分可以出现$r$种颜色、奇部分可以出现$s$种颜色的过配分。他们提出了关于该函数在特定算术级数上模2幂次的同余猜想，需要对这些猜想进行证明。

Method: 使用初等证明方法，仅依赖于经典的$q$-级数操作和Ramanujan theta函数的性质，不涉及复杂的模形式理论。

Result: 成功证明了Thejitha和Fathima提出的所有关于$\bar{a}_{r,s}(n)$模2幂次的同余猜想，验证了这些猜想对于特定整数$r,s$和算术级数的正确性。

Conclusion: 通过初等$q$-级数方法证明了过着色配分函数的同余性质，为这类组合函数的模性质研究提供了简洁有效的证明途径。

Abstract: In a recent paper, Thejitha and Fathima introduced the overcolored partition function $\bar{a}_{r,s}(n)$, which enumerates overpartitions in which even parts may appear in one of $r$ colors and odd parts in one of $s$ colors, for fixed integers $r,s \geq 1$. They also proposed several conjectures concerning families of congruences modulo powers of $2$ for specific arithmetic progressions of $\bar{a}_{r,s}(n)$. In this paper, we provide an elementary proof of this conjecture that relies only on classical $q$-series manipulations and properties of Ramanujan's theta function.

</details>


### [107] [A geometric proof of Lagrange's theorem for continued fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12425)
*Anton Lukyanenko,Joseph Vandehey*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文将正规连分数的有限展开和最终周期展开的经典结果推广到各种Iwasawa连分数，建立了点性质与模群元素类型之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正规连分数理论中，有限展开对应有理数，最终周期展开对应二次无理数（拉格朗日定理）。作者希望将这些经典结果推广到更广泛的Iwasawa连分数框架中，包括实数、复数、3D、四元数、八元数和海森堡连分数。

Method: 采用适当的模群M，将点的性质与模群元素的类型联系起来：有限展开点对应M(∞)中的点，也对应M中抛物变换的不动点；最终周期点对应M中斜航元素的不动点。使用克利福德代数形式体系将这些点解释为非退化二次方程的根。

Result: 证明了对于适当的模群M，以下三个条件等价：(1)点p有有限展开；(2)p∈M(∞)；(3)p是M中抛物变换的不动点。最终周期点恰好对应M中斜航元素的不动点，这些点可解释为非退化二次方程的根。这为最近整数实数连分数和Hurwitz复数连分数提供了新的拉格朗日定理几何证明。

Conclusion: 成功将正规连分数的经典特征推广到各种Iwasawa连分数，建立了点的展开性质与模群元素类型之间的深刻联系。这种几何方法不仅统一了不同数系中的连分数理论，还为理解恒等式i+1/i=0的推广提供了新视角。

Abstract: For regular continued fractions (CFs), points with finite expansions are exactly the rationals and, by Lagrange's theorem, points with eventually-periodic expansions are exactly the roots of non-degenerate quadratic equations with integer coefficients. We extend both results to proper and discrete Iwasawa CFs, including real, complex, 3D, quaternionic, octonionic, and Heisenberg CFs. Namely, the following three conditions are equivalent for a point $p$: $p$ has a finite expansion, $p\in \mathcal M(\infty)$ for the appropriate modular group $\mathcal M$, and $p$ is a fixed point of a parabolic transformation in $\mathcal M$. Eventually-periodic points correspond exactly to fixed points of loxodromic elements of $\mathcal M$, which can be interpreted as roots of non-degenerate quadratics using the Clifford Algebra formalism of Ahlfors. In particular, this provides a new geometric proof of Lagrange's theorem for nearest-integer real CFs and Hurwitz complex CFs. Lastly, we comment on generalizations of the identity $i+1/i=0$.

</details>


### [108] [A vanishing theorem for residual Eisenstein cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12472)
*Sam Mundy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究半单群在实域上保持极大的极大抛物子群下的剩余Eisenstein上同调，证明剩余表示在中间度上下各一度有上同调类，但中间度以上的类在全自守上同调中消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半单群的剩余Eisenstein上同调，特别关注在实域上保持极大的极大抛物子群情形。目标是理解这些剩余表示的上同调性质及其在全自守上同调中的行为。

Method: 通过构造显式的上链来证明中间度以上类的消失，该上链为剩余表示的非平凡上同调类像提供原像。同时详细研究相关诱导表示的阿基米德分量，证明其包含两个离散级数的子表示，并描述其Harish-Chandra参数，证明交织算子在子表示上恰好有1阶零点。

Result: 在一般假设下，证明剩余表示在中间度减1和加1处有上同调类，但中间度以上的类在全自守上同调中消失。构造了取值于正则Eisenstein级数的显式上链作为原像。确定了诱导表示阿基米德分量的结构，包含两个离散级数的子表示，交织算子在该子表示上恰好有1阶零点。

Conclusion: 半单群在实域上保持极大的极大抛物子群下的剩余Eisenstein上同调具有特定的度分布：剩余表示在中间度上下各一度有上同调类，但中间度以上的类在全自守上同调中消失。通过构造显式原像和详细分析阿基米德分量结构，揭示了这些上同调类的精确行为。

Abstract: We study the residual Eisenstein cohomology of semisimple groups in the context of maximal parabolic subgroups which remain maximal over $\mathbb{R}$. Under certain general hypotheses, we show that these residual representations are cohomological one degree below middle, and one above; however, the classes above middle vanish in the full automorphic cohomology.
  The proof of this vanishing finds an explicit cochain which provides a primitive to the image of a nontrivial class from the cohomology of such a residual representation. This cochain is valued in regular Eisenstein series. Along the way, we study in detail the archimedean component of the relevant induced representation. In particular, we prove that it has a subrepresentation which is the sum of two discrete series, whose Harish-Chandra parameters we describe, and that the intertwining operator vanishes to order exactly $1$ on that subrepresentation.

</details>


### [109] [Derived geometric Satake equivalence on the Beilinson-Drinfeld Grassmannian with one leg in mixed characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12542)
*Katsuyuki Bando*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Fargues-Fontaine曲线上单腿局部Hecke栈的导出几何Satake等价


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fargues-Scholze为Fargues-Fontaine曲线上的几何Langlands纲领建立了框架，并证明了模空间上的几何Satake等价。本文旨在推广这一结果，建立其导出版本。

Method: 通过证明局部Hecke栈的导出范畴与L-群等变完美复形范畴之间的等价性，其中后者定义在对称代数上，该对称代数由移位且(-1)-Tate扭曲的李代数构成。

Result: 证明了单腿情况下，局部Hecke栈的平展上同调导出范畴等价于L-群在对称代数上的等变完美复形范畴，该对称代数对应移位且(-1)-Tate扭曲的李代数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Fargues-Fontaine曲线上几何Langlands纲领的导出几何Satake等价，为Fargues-Scholze框架提供了重要的导出范畴推广。

Abstract: Fargues-Scholze developed a framework for the geometric Langlands program on the Fargues-Fontaine curve. In particular, they proved the geometric Satake equivalence on the moduli space of closed Cartier divisors on the curve. We prove the derived version of this equivalence with one leg. Namely, we show that the derived category of etale sheaves on the local Hecke stack is equivalent to the category of L-group-equivariant perfect complexes over the symmetric algebra of the shifted and (-1)-Tate-twisted Lie algebra.

</details>


### [110] [Disproof of the uniform Littlewood conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12611)
*Johannes Schleischitz*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文证明最近提出的均匀Littlewood猜想(ULC)是错误的，反例构成剩余集，且Hausdorff维数至少为1。同时否定了p-adic均匀Littlewood问题及更一般S-算术设置的某些扭曲版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Bandi、Fregoli和Kleinbock最近提出的均匀Littlewood猜想(ULC)的有效性，探索其在各种算术设置下的真伪性，包括p-adic版本和更一般的S-算术设置。

Method: 采用半构造性方法：非构造性部分利用Bourgain和Kontorovich关于Zaremba猜想的有效结果以及有限域上乘积集基数的估计；构造性部分通过构建反例来证明猜想不成立。

Result: 1. 证明均匀Littlewood猜想(ULC)是错误的，反例构成剩余集；2. 对于扭曲问题，Hausdorff维数至少为1；3. 否定了p-adic均匀Littlewood问题；4. 对于任何真子集S(可能无限)的素数，否定了更一般S-算术设置的某些扭曲版本。

Conclusion: 均匀Littlewood猜想不成立，这与经典非均匀情况形成对比。结果揭示了均匀版本与经典版本在算术性质上的本质差异，特别是在S-算术设置中，当S至少有两个元素时经典版本成立，但均匀版本不成立。

Abstract: We show that the uniform Littlewood Conjecture (ULC) recently introduced by Bandi, Fregoli and Kleinbock is false. More precisely the counterexamples form a residual set, the method further suggests positive Hausdorff dimension. For a mildly twisted problem, we indeed separately show that the Hausdorff dimension is at least $1$. Moreover, we disprove a uniform version of the $p$-adic Littlewood problem, as well as some twisted weaker version of a more general $S$-arithmetic setting, for any proper subset (possible infinite) of primes $S$. The latter contrasts the classical (non-uniform) case where the answer is known to be affirmative when $S$ has at least two elements. The disproof of ULC, our main new result, is semi-constructive; the non-constructive part involves effective results on Zaremba's famous conjecture by Bourgain and Kontorovich, as well as estimates for the cardinality of product sets over finite fields.

</details>


### [111] [The weight two and opposite sign cases for the Fourier relative trace formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13062)
*Matteo Di Scipio*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文通过阿德尔相对迹公式方法证明了Petersson/Bruggeman-Kuznetsov公式，在特定参数条件下获得精化版本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法证明PBK公式存在局限性，需要更一般化的证明框架。作者旨在通过阿德尔相对迹公式这一更强大的工具，在更广泛的条件下获得精化的PBK公式，为相关数论研究提供新视角。

Method: 采用阿德尔相对迹公式方法，在非阿基米德测试函数f满足几何假设和谱假设的条件下，分别处理κ=2的全纯情形和m₁m₂<0的非全纯情形，推导出精化的PBK公式。

Result: 成功证明了在几何假设和谱假设下，对于κ=2的全纯情形和m₁m₂<0的非全纯情形，可以获得精化的Petersson/Bruggeman-Kuznetsov公式，为相关迹公式研究提供了新工具。

Conclusion: 阿德尔相对迹公式方法是证明PBK公式的有效工具，在特定假设下可以获得精化版本，这一方法有望推广到更一般的情形，为数论中的迹公式研究开辟新途径。

Abstract: We provide an adelic relative trace formula proof to the Petersson/Bruggeman-Kuznetsov (PBK) formulas, specifically in the holomorphic case for $κ=2$ and the non-holomorphic case for $m_1m_2<0$. Given two sets of hypothesis on the non archimedean test function $f$, called the geometric and spectral assumptions, this approach allows us to obtain refined PBK formulas.

</details>


### [112] [Jacobian graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13198)
*Arthur Forey,Javier Fresán,Emmanuel Kowalski,Yuval Wigderson*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 提出雅可比图，这是一种正则图族，其谱特征与随机图无法区分，但局部结构却与随机图截然不同


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有随机图谱特征但局部结构完全不同的图族，以探索谱图论中谱特征与局部结构之间的关系

Method: 利用曲线的广义雅可比簇的几何性质，以及有限域上特征和的一般等分布定理来构造雅可比图

Result: 成功构造了雅可比图族，这些图在谱特征上与随机图无法区分，但具有完全不同的局部结构特征

Conclusion: 雅可比图提供了谱特征与局部结构可以解耦的明确例子，对谱图论和伪随机图理论有重要意义

Abstract: We introduce jacobian graphs, which are explicit families of regular graphs that are spectrally indistinguishable from random graphs, but whose local structure is very different from that of random graphs. The construction relies on the geometric properties of generalized jacobians of curves and on general equidistribution theorems for character sums over finite fields.

</details>


### [113] [Sizes of Pre-Images of the Minimal Euclidean Function on the Gaussian Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13225)
*Hester Graves*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用高斯整数最小欧几里得函数的几何描述，推导其原像集大小的计算公式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者在2023年首次提出了非平凡数域的可计算最小欧几里得函数，并给出了高斯整数上最小欧几里得函数φ_ℤ[i]的公式及其原像集φ_ℤ[i]^{-1}([0,n])的几何描述。本文旨在利用该几何构造来证明原像集大小的精确公式。

Method: 基于先前工作中建立的高斯整数最小欧几里得函数φ_ℤ[i]的几何描述，作者使用该构造来分析和计算原像集φ_ℤ[i]^{-1}([0,n])的大小。方法涉及几何论证和组合计数技术。

Result: 本文证明了高斯整数最小欧几里得函数原像集大小的精确公式，即|φ_ℤ[i]^{-1}([0,n])|的计算表达式。这些公式提供了该函数值分布的量化和结构信息。

Conclusion: 通过利用先前建立的几何构造，本文成功推导并证明了高斯整数最小欧几里得函数原像集大小的精确计算公式，深化了对该函数值分布特征的理解。

Abstract: In 2023, the author presented the first computable minimal Euclidean function for a non-trivial number field. Along with a formula for $φ_{\mathbb{Z}[i]}$, the minimal Euclidean function on the Gaussian inteers, the same paper introduced a geometric description for $φ_{\mathbb{Z}[i]}^{-1}([0,n])$. This paper uses that construction to prove formulas for the size of the function's pre-images, or $|φ_{\mathbb{Z}[i]}^{-1}([0,n])|$.

</details>
